## Wave 22 — all 4 lanes landed (`46abcb3db`)

- **Optic-lane scratch pooling** (hitches F1): `fireDiscipline.opticLaneBodiesFlat` + `aiFireIntent.opticLaneBodiesWithShelved`/`planOpticBankShot` rebuilt per-shooter-tick flat `{x,z,radius,entity}` rows, an indexOf `Map`, and a `{values(){}}` shelved wrapper — N energy-armed NPCs × C collidables → per-tick allocs inside `update()`. All three are now WeakMap-pooled (rows re-stamped per call, `indexOf.clear()`+refill, wrapper keyed on `live`+`shelved` identity). The byte-exactness constraint (`traceOpticRay` shares the corpus with `opticField`; `collides` flips both directions post-spawn at 8 sites with no version bump) rules out version-latching the corpus — the rows re-test `splinterBody` at consume time, so the scratch is alloc-free AND corpus-identical. `traceOpticRay` signature untouched.
- **`flavorCarriers` counter-only lane** (hitches F3): new index lane bumped in `appendEntityIndex`/`removeEntityIndex`/`removeEntitiesFromIndex` on the append-time-decidable predicate `data.flavorTargetRef|flavorSourceId != null` (zero post-spawn stamping sites; all carriers `type:'fx'`). `bandRadio._scanLandmarkCarriers` latches on `entityIndexLaneVersion(LANDMARK_CARRIER_LANES)` with a `<0` rescan guard — the 0.2 s cadence O(entities) census no longer re-runs under unrelated churn. Chosen over the audit's `index.fx`-domain suggestion: the lane bump is type-agnostic, so a hypothetical non-fx carrier still triggers a rescan where the bucket-domain fix would silently miss it.
- **Ceres causal actor probes** (hitches F6): `audioSystem._ceresCausalActorPosition` O(entityList)/event → freighter-ledger probe → `indexedWorldRecordEntity` → fallback walk; `traffic._ceresCausalWorldRecordIdForSlot` factored to exported `ceresActivityActorWorldRecordId` (cast-slot → `ceres:activity:` prefix → `stableRecordId` convoy id).
- **Index-bucket retargets** (hitches F7): `cloak.cloakCandidateEntities` → `indexedShipLikeOrEntitiesScan`; `salvageActions` INF-U19 hot-core ship-clear → `pickups`; `swarmArena` wall-blocker census → `asteroids`; `automation` pre-dressing fx walk → `fx`. Skipped two retargets on byte-exactness grounds: `entityResolver` byStationId walk (first-match order vs index's alive-filtered iteration under dead entities differs) and `heistFacilities` dual-predicate first-match (ordering not preserved).
- **Overlay-union pending stamps** (lod F1): multi-part trade-hub stations (helios/tethys/drift/reach) classified ~3× narrower than their composed silhouette — the deterministic faction overlay (`var_station_trade_hub_{free,mts,scn}_overlay_v01`) instantiates at the same draw scale but its census row was absent from every envelope stamp. New `placeVisualSizeWithOverlay` unions the overlay row (component-wise max, both at the same scale) into `stampPendingPlaceVisualBounds`'s two branches (committedX + visualBounds) and the compile-window re-stamp — while the scale basis stays on the base record (the commit resolves scale on the record's own envelope). Composed measure ~549 documented at `partsLibrary.js:3964-3969`; the union covers up to the overlay-inclusive envelope (~212 vs ~180 for scn) — full coverage needs composed-envelope census rows, deferred M-effort.
- **TargetRadius-branch committedX** (lod F2): Z-dominant POIs (Resonant Cathedral landmark family) stamped only `standInFit=diameter` — the resolving marker drew `diameter` wide for a body committing at `diameter·x0/max`. Now stamps `boundaryResolvingCommittedX` from `modelTruthRowForEntity` + `resolvePlaceDrawScale` — marker arm reads the honest committed extent.
- **Frozen-boundary save machinery** (boot F1/F2): `_frozenTickBoundary()` (paused OR skip-full-tick) gates both directions — `_serializeDataFrozenAsync` rechecks mid-yield and refuses capture if the boundary opened (a stale rollback is worse than the win) — and the new `save:loadSpeculationTarget` bus event: pause + save-load screens emit their slot, `speculateLoadPrepare` resolves+prepares the envelope against `_saveStoreGeneration` during menu dwell, `load()` consumes `spec.raw` only when the generation check proves same-disk-bytes. Pause's quit-load and save-load's pick now warm the envelope read behind the veil.
- **Custody + preload residue** (boot F3–F6): `_rebindPersistedFreightCustodyCarriers` early-outs when `dir.stats.openFreightCustodies` is empty (the common case — the O(entities) walk ran unconditionally per load) and rides `indexedShipLikeOrEntitiesScan` when custodies exist; `@elemaudio/web-renderer`, `rapierCollisionWorld` (serial cold-fetch inside the physics-authority gate for New Game), and `HDRLoader` (fallback path cold fetch) are modulepreloaded.
- **Deferred**: hitches F2 (`nearBodyCandidatesFor` — `collides!==false` census vs `===true` bucket predicate, unresolved post-spawn flip caveat), F4 (lifetimeSweep — removal timing is iteration-visible; kill-queue misses direct flag writes, audit itself recommends KEEP), F5 (`observeProjectileReflection` hostile walk — no bucket covers the union incl. hostile projectiles), boot F7 (emit slicing — documented predecessor-drain landmine), lod F1-full (composed-envelope census rows, M).

Verify: golden `47a` run bit-identical (`e517a97b…`, deterministic true); modulepreload registry in sync (32 main + 175); focused suites green (optic beam/shelved lanes, indexed ship-like scan, audio ceres cue/work, gfx12 standin, swarm deferred-warm, bounded-autosave, save-envelope-fidelity, structural-preload, automation-outpost-presence); `pause-freezes-ship-presentation` red A/B-verified preexisting on clean HEAD.

## Wave 25 — popin + boot-stub lanes landed (`3fe4c1e63`)

- **Unfiled-jump warm arm** (popin F1): `jump:unfiledConfirmed` now arms `beginIncomingSectorPrewarm` + `warmLiveSectorFullExtras` — an unfiled jump's destination only exists at confirm (`chargeStart`'s `targetSectorId: null` made the earlier arm no-op), so the whole post-confirm charge window (~3.5-8 s) now decodes the return-sector census + FULL-extras cohort instead of paying it inside `enterSector`.
- **Roster role rename** (popin F2): the sector-full-extras warm passed a residency role matching NEITHER `deadlineClass` (`/runway|deadline|sector-prewarm|sector-prepared/i`) nor `WARM_PURPOSE_RESIDENCY_ROLE` — roster hulls were posting ambient-class behind the very decode they're meant to precede. Renamed `sector-full-extras-decode-runway` so both classifiers match.
- **Membership-dwell warm** (popin F3): new `sector:membershipCandidate` emit at the Voronoi latch (world.js) + presentation-tier renderer arm — a continuous crossing's promote cohort (dressing, ambient pool, boss) used to decode inside the enter frame; now the 8 s dwell decodes it (stub no-ops without a materialized REDUCED bag).
- **Envelope full-extras manifest** (popin F4): new `saveEnvelopeFullExtrasStubs(data)` — the menu-dwell warm previously missed the saved sector's restore-time ambient pool / zone-presence / boss / ecology growth / machine rings (those lived only in the live-bag enumerator). Fresh-materialize epoch-0 mirrors `_ensureSectorMaterializedChunks`; ambient re-roll gated on no durable combat history, boss on undefeated + unclaimed.

### In-session W27 regression-verify fixes (`93d336958` + `18f9df519`)

- **Journal destroy-suppression edge** (`93d336958`): `_noMesh` is mutable post-spawn (renderer latches it at :14911 after build failures, clears at world-context change), so `isEntityJournaled` can flip false mid-life — a journaled entity despawned while flagged had its destroy suppressed, leaving a stale retained journal slot. `recordDestroy` now also honors a `journaledEntities` WeakSet stamped at `publishSpawn` — suppression applies only to never-journaled entities, no `ensureEntityId` allocation on the suppressed path.
- **Claims blocker-domain coverage** (`18f9df519`): the F8 spatial-hash path narrowed the blocker domain — a `station|asteroid` type filter wrongly excluded other blocker types (mine/bomb/fx/place prop…) and the uncovered list was built from `index.statics` instead of the walk's real domain (`entityList` filtered by the blocker). Now: no type filter (the blocker applies the domain), uncovered = entityList members with falsy-but-not-`false` `collides`, `maxR` over all entityList radii, and the whole fast path gated on provable index coverage (`entities.size === _indexedIds.size`). `alienEcology` juvenile promotion — the only post-spawn *entity* radius growth — bumps `collidesFlipEpoch` so radius-derived caches invalidate.

**W33 popin** — **Cosmetic overflow floor** (F1): kill-burst overflow trim now sheds cosmetic presentation slices only down to a 16-slice floor, then the priority lane sheds its own oldest — `entity:spawned` tails carry once-only stamps (`materializeT0`, `spiralDone` for recycled wreck ids) so the old cosmetic-first cap could permanently cancel an entity's materialize ramp at the busiest visible moment (a dropped `entity:destroyed` tail, by contrast, self-heals via the residency poll). **Law-incident dispatch warm** (F2): `warmLawIncidentDispatchDecode` polls `state.lawSecurity.incidents` once per residency pass — a distress/responding incident announces a patrol ETA then mints reserve `patrol_lawman` hulls cold at `dispatchAt`; now the responder hull (archetype + `incident.factionId`, matching `_dispatchIncident`'s pick) warms while `dispatchAt` sits inside `TABLE_DECODE_RUNWAY_SECONDS`, WeakMap-deduped per incident. **Sandbox embark speculation** (F3): the shipped sandbox front door emitted no speculation — preset-card hover/focus now arms `game:embarkSpeculation` during real menu dwell and `requestSandboxGame` emits the final config (`cfg.sectorId` covered when the scenario retargets, else NEW_GAME default). **`bus.clear()` priority leak** (F4): `presentationPriorityQueue` now drops on `clear()` — post-clear phantom lifecycle presents closed; contract test extended.

**W33 boot** — **Gesture-arm coalesce** (F1): `_emitEmbarkSpec` deferred through `setTimeout` (not `queueMicrotask`, which drains pre-paint) so a seed-typing burst arms once after the gesture instead of enumerating ~60–100 warm posts per keystroke inside the handler; `_prefetchEmbarkVisuals` gained a 2 s `(sectorId,seed,shipDefId)` signature latch — identical re-arms (starter re-pick, onShow, sandbox hover repeats) skip the whole enumeration while spaced re-arms still re-pin evicted warms. **`run_reset` roster clears** (F2): both `entity:destroyed` clear emits (sliced `prepareRun`, unsliced `discardPreparedNewGameScene`) carry `reason:'run_reset'`; audio `_onDestroyed` early-returns on it — no explosion cluster + station hush playing behind the veil against the stale player position. **rng seam hygiene** (F3): `randomSeedText` uses `Math.random` as its own comment documents (was preferring `ctx.state.rng`, sharing the boot stream). **Covered-hit twin gate** (F5): `captureWorldSitePayloadState` only suppresses the fallback walk on a failed-predicate index hit when `byWorldRecordIdCount` proves the row unique — a mark→sweep corpse or wrong twin no longer yields a transiently absent capture. **Deterministic ward pick** (F6): `ward_screen` now selects the lowest entity id among valid screeners — the `shipLike` append order and `entityList` swap-pop order diverged on first-match picks (the class W22 doctrine documents). **Hoist middle-range fill** (F7): `hoistDeadlineGlassMeshBuilds` stamps the provably non-glass `(firstNonGlassIdx, i)` range 0 at inversion instead of re-evaluating those predicates in pass 2.

`ship_wasp` needs no arm (REQUIRED_WHOLE_SHIP). **Gate-wing dedupe fix** (caveat): the dedupe key was `{sectorId}|{to}|{day}` — widened to `|{factionId}|{security}|{wanted}` so a verdict that flips inputs re-plans instead of being suppressed by the stale-day stamp.

**W35 hitches** — **Post-parse compile pacing** (F1): `scheduleGltfParse` paced parse *starts* but the `dedupeGltfTextureSources`→`compileBlueprint` continuation ran inline on whichever microtask drain the worker replies clustered into — a 6–10-file burst stacked ~10–30 ms of synchronous compile in one frame. New `scheduleGltfCompile` in `decodeTaskBudget.js`: three FIFO class lanes (visible > deadline > ambient, mirroring the decode budget), 2 compiles per animation frame, re-arms until empty (headless hosts run immediately). The tail claims the caller's class; `deadlineJoinedAssetTasks` WeakSet marks ambient tasks a deadline caller joined so their compile tail promotes alongside the budget flush; owner-departure is re-checked inside the queued tail so a departed owner skips the expensive stage; the cached-blueprint fast path never reaches this code (admit layer resolves first). **Speculative warm class demotion** (F2): `wanted-tier` (roster + modular pool) and `gate-wing` warms now post at ambient-warm class — they're speculation that can expire unused (heat dip / bounty paid / verdict flip) yet sat FIFO-equal with genuine deadline demand; a real deadline join re-grades the cached task's tail via `deadlineJoin` + `promote`, so speculation never queues ahead of real demand. Committed countdown arms (depot-watch/patrol, unique-wreck complications, planet challenge, lane ambush, station patrol) keep the deadline runway class — those spawns fire on a timer, not on player mercy. F3 ranked the surviving inline emit handlers — all under the measured-cost bar; no work item.

## Wave 36 — lod + boot landed (`45a64243a`); popin + hitches auditing

**W36 lod** — **Compile-tail join promotion** (F1): the compile class was frozen at push — an ambient warm whose doc resolved queued its tail ambient, and a later deadline/visible joiner (sharing the cached task) promoted decode waiters but still awaited the ambient-positioned tail; `deadlineJoinedAssetTasks` (WeakSet, class-less) capped even a *visible* joiner at 'deadline'. Now `joinedAssetTaskClasses` (WeakMap\<task,class\>) carries the joiner's own class, and `regradeGltfCompile(token, class)` splice-promotes a still-queued entry to the HEAD of the target lane — covering joins that land between enqueue and drain. **Time-boxed compile drain** (F2): `GLTF_COMPILE_FRAME_LIMIT = 2` → `GLTF_COMPILE_FRAME_MS = 4`; tasks run synchronously inside the drain so the measured cost is the compile itself (the batch-dispatch idiom could never see fn cost) — small-part bursts drain ~2–5× faster while the per-frame worst case stays budget + one compile, min one task per frame. **Pick verdict memoization** (F3): `admitNextUpgradeJob`'s comparator called `authoredUpgradePriority` (→ `entityIsOnAuthoredGlassBand` + `entityIsOnscreen` with `camera.updateMatrixWorld` + `setFromProjectionMatrix` per call) per pair — O(n·log n) heavy verdicts per sector-arrival admit. Per-job memo (`pickVerdicts` Map) decorated across the comparator and the two in-pick `.some` scans; the deferred `onAuthoredPipelineStaged` callback intentionally still reads live glass state.

**W36 boot** — **Render-package tail pacing** (F1): `renderPackageLoader`'s post-decode continuation — `buildInstancePlan` traverse + `prepareDecoded`→`prepareRenderPackageBlueprint` (per-vertex layout rebake) + `createLoadedPackage` + `registerAsset`, all synchronous — was the dominant decode path and never got `scheduleGltfCompile`; ~60–100 embark packages' tails could stack in one microtask drain. Now routed through the same class lanes at `activeDecodeClass()` — the caller's flag window holds through settle, so joined tasks classify at the joiner's class with zero plumbing. **Opening set admissionVisible** (F2): `requestOpeningCompositionUpgrades` now posts `{ admissionVisible: true }` — the ready latch previously ranked deadline, FIFO behind its own speculative backfill; the hook re-grades an in-flight job on each repeat call. **Ambush warm cell gap** (F3): `warmLaneAmbushDecode` dropped `payload.sectorId !== currentSectorId` — the arm emits at ≤2400 WU with sectorId from the segment midpoint's Voronoi cell, and mid-chord the player can sit one cell over, so the gate suppressed the warm (and the 20 s re-arm blocked retry) exactly when the ambush fired seconds later on the bisector cross. **paintWarm budget** (F5): the forced style+layout pass is timed like `prewarm` — a paint over 6 ms yields a frame before the next warm so two heavy screens never chain inside one flight-idle slice. **Embark speculation arms** (F6): crucible Practice-room door arms on pointerenter/focus (fixed sling preset, real dwell lead) and the demo results plate's "Take it to the belt" door arms the fresh NEW_GAME target while the plate sits.

CI triage on `18f2d8123`: every shard red classified — sim's 12-check set, static(1-3), and `feel`'s `crucible-thirty-wave-arc` (7≠6 wave-11 + 9≠7 wave-20) all reproduce identically on clean master `ac1b580cf` (upstream canon); `check-ui-budgets` staleness verified upstream-owned (227 files diverge on clean master vs the branch's 111 — upstream's own post-`954228479` commits invalidated its re-minted baseline); browser(3) `check-station-tabs` + browser(4) `check-mission-handoff` reds are the 15s dock-budget flake class — both green locally on master AND branch.

**Real find**: `check-mission-accept-handoff-runtime` exposed a latent probe-design race. Its synthetic `btn.click()` on `[data-accept]` arms the dossier's assistive-tech auto-hold (`acceptHoldMs` 450 + ~260ms retract). On frame-starved hosts (master ~1.9fps docked) the hold completes *after* the probe's ~1s poll window; on live-frame hosts (branch ~8fps docked — the presentation/drain pump stays live) `acceptMission` correctly consumes the offer *inside* the window, and the probe's last-attempt `commit.found` assert can no longer see it. Traced end-to-end via property-setter stacks: `acceptMission` → `ui:acceptMission` → `drainAndCommit` → the hold firing on our dossier — the accept path works correctly on both trees. Probe repair: when the accept control is consumed mid-poll, the mission's own accept evidence (active growth + tracked resolution — the same contract the downstream wait asserts) now proves the control worked. The `commit.disabled` assert is gated on `found`. Verified: the check now runs to its final assert on branch; residual `__spaceface_player_store` ERR_ABORTED noise is identical on clean master (local teardown artifact).

## Wave 41 — lod + popin landed (this commit)

**W41 lod** (lane `7182f591`) — `saturated: false`, all 3 findings are priority-taxonomy gaps on the same runway law:

- **F1 runway-inbound stall bypass** (`partsLibrary.js` `queuedGlassLawJobStillNeeded`): the steady-ship-pass predicate only honored the ≤1.5 on-glass grade, so a hull due inside `TABLE_PROMOTE_HORIZON_SECONDS` held behind a wedged non-ship sat the full 120 s `AUTHORED_UPGRADE_NONSHIP_STALL_MS` — then aborted, re-admitted ≤3 strikes, and crossed the glass as a marker. The predicate now accepts the runway-inbound clause, and the five-type gates at rung-5 grade (:5643) + serial release (:6838) + the new bypass clause all read one shared helper pair — `entityRidesAuthoredRunway` (type domain) + `entityIsAuthoredRunwayInbound` (type + horizon via the same `willEntityEnterAuthoredUpgradeRunway` the renderer's relevance classifier ends on). Bounded: bypass still needs every in-flight stalled; busy-place pass still caps active ≤ limit.
- **F2 creator-side consumer liveness** (`assetLoader.js:727-732`): the creator registered `isResidencyOwnerActive` only `if typeof === 'function'` — an ownerless warm (e.g. `warmEnemyRosterDecode`, which passes only `{residencyRole, sectorId}`) registered zero creator predicates, so its last joiner's death resolved the compile tail `null` and evicted the shared decode — the exact warm the task existed to pre-stage. The creator now always registers the composed `() => authoredConsumerIsActive(options)` — ownerless defaults live, real owners keep their exact predicate, aborted signals still read dead.
- **F3 `'fx'` + geology rung-5 grade** (`entityRidesAuthoredRunway`): `'fx'` place-props (`wrapPlacePropWithAuthoredPart`) and `hasExplicitAuthoredGeologyPresentation` entities ride the serial queue but were absent from the 5-type gates — grading ambient-10, starving behind ambient arrivals, and popping on approach. Both now in the shared gate, so rung-5 grade, runway bypass, and release clause cover them identically.

**W41 popin** (lane `db179630`) — `saturated: false`. **F1 census-arm providers** (9 systems + opening invoke): `sector:enter` spawners that materialize bodies synchronously (wingmen, aftermathWrecks, uniqueWrecks, survivorPod, traffic, missions, aceMemory challenge scheduling, intervention pendings, factionPresence) each registered a `sectorCookProvider` mirroring its own listener body — the vesper pattern. Jump census and the opening cook (`renderer.js:10024-10032`) now invoke providers deterministically before `collectFirstFlightCookEntities`, so enter-spawns land inside the composition set instead of on listener-registration order. Providers read the sector arg or `world.currentSectorId` and are idempotent (handlers dedupe); systems with teardown paths (survivorPod/aftermathWrecks null-guard, factionPresence `_unsub` gate, aceMemory `_subs` gate) no-op after destroy. Nemesis deliberately unpaced-provider (its pacing is authored), consistent with the lane's exemption.

- **F1 handoff-window micro-batch admit**: covered on-glass rows serialized one admission per rAF through the whole `firstFlightHandoffHold` window — with N stacked readable-glass jobs the last showed its pending substrate for ~(N−1)× lane latency while its decode was already paid. When the next pick is a readable-glass job whose `prefetchResolved` flag is set (the handoff priming loop's prefetchPromise already settled — exactly the covered population), the admit grants one bounded extra slot under `AUTHORED_UPGRADE_OPENING_LIMIT`; steady concurrency untouched. New `job.prefetchResolved` tracked at both prefetchPromise resolution sites.
- **F2 null-sector payload wipe**: `_onSectorEnter`'s `p.sector`-null early-return sat BELOW the `continuous`/`repeatedCeresEntry`/`_cleanup()` block — a sector-less synthetic payload could wipe `_active` unstamped. The sector resolution + spawn-helper guard now hoist above the cleanup path; `requestedSectorId` falls back `p.sectorId || currentSectorId || 'unknown'`.
- **F3 unconditional morrow drone stub**: `saveEnvelopeSectorWarm` pushed `{type:'drone'}` for every morrow sector — but morrow mounts procedural `buildMorrowVisual` and never reads `place_mining_drone.glb` (dead over-coverage; automation-drone groups DO mount packaged bodies via `buildDrone`→`attachPackagedBody`, kept). Explicit-authored payloads already pin via `entityRequiresAuthoredPresentation`.

## Wave 44 — lod lane (this commit)

- **F1 enter-dedupe stamp hole**: CONFIRMED the mechanism the `495b06c6f` `newGame()` clear already fixes — sector objects are `SECTOR_BY_ID` catalog singletons (`world.js:324`), so `(p.sector === _emittedSector)` still matches after `resetRunState` replaces `state.world` wholesale; with `enterSerial` restarting at 1, a run that emitted exactly one enter would suppress the next run's opening enter entirely (no `_resetRngForSector`, no authored cast pockets, zero ambient traffic until first jump). `traffic.newGame()` runs on the fresh-run path via `resetFreshRunSystems` (`runReset.js` FRESH_RUN_SYSTEMS) — the clear closes it.
- **F2 TABLE_INBOUND_APPROACH_WU 600→1200**: the ~550-velocity model under-covered two real exceeders — Ceres-sling flings exit at 3× combat cruise (hawser drive_torch_l 320 → ~960 WU/s frozen at shelve, advanced ballistically with no drag decay) and the catalog's absolute propulsion bound is torch_l `travelCeiling` 1120. A 960-row closes ~200-350 WU/s faster than the disc modeled — ~2-4s less runway than the 13.5s budget, promotable before its authored hull compiles → stand-in on the magic frame. Constant raised past the true ceiling with margin; bound stays `FAR_ROW_BUDGET=128` + grid.
- **F3 admit-time runway re-grade**: `jobStillNeeded` was the sole admit gate — it checks scene/alive/mesh-ownership but never re-evaluates runway relevance, so a transient graze (request side deliberately over-admits "request broad, grade tight") ran a dead compose+residency cycle through the serial slot the genuinely-inbound hulls wait on. New `jobRunwayRegradeStillWanted` at the admit point: runway-class jobs (`entityRidesAuthoredRunway`) evict unless they carry glass law — `options.admissionVisible`, `authoredUpgradePriority ≤ 1.5`, `entityIsOnReadableGlass` — or the tight grade-side predicate still says inbound at 1.5× `authoredRunwayHorizonSeconds` (hysteresis against rim-skimming oscillation). Unmeasurable geometry (missing pos) keeps the slot — no evidence of departure is not evidence. Evicted jobs re-arm cleanly: cancel clears `byBoundary`/`byKey`/`authoredUpgradePromise`, and the request-side `pending` dedupe lapses on promise settle.

## W46 — popin + boot lanes (this commit)

All four W46 lanes independently converged on F1 (landed `9214cd1f6`).

- **popin F2 continuous-seam hold uncovered-row bound**: covered exempt rows still rode the 8/frame count-budgeted pump — cheap dressing mounts bind on count before the slice clock, so a dressing-heavy seam on a loaded host finished mounting after the 1.5s blend and popped in over the next frames. The hold now compares `_holdExemptRemaining` against `framesLeft × RUNTIME_MESH_BUILD_BUDGET` each frame; when the uncovered count outruns the remaining budget it drains the hoisted exempt prefix with a remainder-sized cap — the admission-slice clock (~3ms) still bounds each burst, so no unbounded dump.
- **popin F3 anchored-rock respawn outside the enter census**: `_repairAnchors` ran only on the 1s accumulator — an anchored rock killed since the last visit respawned up to a second after the census froze, mounting past the enter window. Now called in the `sector:enter` listener and the `sectorCookProviders` callback (both `!_worldRestoreActive`-guarded); idempotent, so the accumulator's later pass is a no-op rescan.
- **boot F2 sliced census stale-sector hazard**: a second `sector:enter` inside a `yieldLiveSectorGpu` gap left the cook running with the captured S1 — its providers then ran cleanup/mint under S2 (`traffic._cookProvider`'s dedupe fails on the sector OBJECT: `_cleanup()` wipes S2's roster, re-mints S1 hulls into live space, stamps S2's epoch so S2 never re-mints). `prepareLiveSectorAfterJump` now carries a generation counter + `currentSectorId` re-check at every yield (provider loop, mesh drain, post-cook); a superseded cook bails with `{skipped:'sector-superseded'}` and skips the shared-flag finally tail, which the newest cook owns. Covers continuous enters too (they never bump the generation but always move `currentSectorId`).
- **boot F3 first-flight prefetch skipped the job-liveness compose**: `primeNextAuthoredAssetPlan`'s first-flight branch called `preloadAuthoredAssetsForEntity` directly — a dropped `firstFlightPrefetchJob`'s late retain revived the owner `cancelQueuedJob` released (the exact hole the W45 compose closed on the general path). Now routes through `startAuthoredJobAssetPrefetch` (null → `Promise.resolve()` keeps the `.then` shape).
- **boot F4 gate-vocabulary gaps**: 'beacon' was absent from `isInboundDecodeHull`, the hold-exempt hull list, and `firstFlightReadableContactKind`; 'payload' was absent from `isInboundDecodeHull`. A packaged beacon/payload inbound during flight missed the approach-radius extension and the readable lanes — mounted as a resolving marker at contact. All four lists now include both types.

- **hitches F1 — chunked cook-provider protocol lands**: `prepareLiveSectorAfterJump`'s provider loop now calls each `sectorCookProvider` for an iterator and drives it serially to completion across the 8ms slice clock (supersede → `iterator.return()`); every provider must mint its dedupe stamp in the first synchronous step and yield only between atomic items. Converted: traffic (per ambient hull + tail), uniqueWrecks (per materialized record), factionPresence (per presence plan), missions (`yield* spawnTargetsForSectorChunked` + tail), asteroidSites (per world site + per anchor row). Emit listeners drain the same generator synchronously — identical roster/RNG order both paths.
- **hitches F2 census staleness at env bake**: `cookStale()` now checked right after `try {` (before `_bakeEnv`) and at the top of each provider iteration, so a cook minted already-stale (second sliced emit force-drains the first's tail after `currentSectorId` flipped) can't run env bake / rock promote / provider[0] on the dead sector — the live hazard was stale `traffic._cookProvider` wiping + reminting wrong-sector hulls stamped with the new serial.
- **hitches F3 provider `.slice()` snapshot**: the provider loop iterated the live `sectorCookProviders` array; vesper splices its own provider on destroy, so a synchronous teardown mid-iteration skipped the next provider. Now iterates a snapshot.
- **hitches F4 `lawWitnessSightBlocked` wrapper**: the flat path allocated a 1-element `[occluders]` wrapper per call per scanner×pod per tick; now iterates `occluders.dynamic ?? occluders` directly (zero alloc).
- **popin F1 repost ping-pong**: rim-grazers oscillate across the runway horizon → `jobRunwayRegradeStillWanted` evicts at prime → repost ~1-2/s forever (the request-side cooloff never binds because the same-entity keep clause is true at request). `restoreBoundaryAfterPreJobRefusal` now counts `regrade-evict-cooloff` restores; at `AUTHORED_REGRADE_REPOST_MAX=3` a request needs `runwayWantedBeyondHorizon` (the runway-wanted clauses minus the horizon) or it refuses again — only a hard-evidence re-approach re-posts.
- **popin F2 persistent-skip stamps**: `_drainMeshBuildQueue`'s per-id stale/irrelevant skips now stamp `_holdExemptPersistentSkips` during a hold, so the protected-drain repartition stops re-triaging them every frame (live → saved/irrelevant is terminal inside the window).
- **popin F3 seam-tail prewarm release was unconditional**: the continuous-hold `===0` tail released whatever sat in `_incomingSectorPrewarm` — a `chargeStart`/`unfiledConfirmed`/`player:death` inside the 1.5s blend mints a different-sector record into the slot, and hold end released the NEW record's pin ~1.5s early. Release is now guarded by `record.sectorId === _sectorHandoffSectorId` (the different-sector record keeps its pin); `_holdExemptPersistentSkips` cleared on seam arm/hold end.
- **boot F1 stale-emit payload guards**: a tail-drained `sector:enter` replayed under a newer enter made materializing listeners mint for the departed sector under the live world's id. Guard: `p.enterEpoch != null && world.enterSerial != null && p.enterEpoch !== world.enterSerial` → return (minted payloads carry the emit's epoch; synthetic/test payloads carry none and always run). Applied at traffic/asteroidSites/aftermathWrecks/survivorPod/factionPresence/missions/heistFacilities/uniqueWrecks/recoveryEncounter. (First-draft `sectorId !== currentSectorId` guard over-reached — suppressed legitimate synthetic enters, caught by `ceres-activity-traffic-cast` — re-based onto the epoch envelope.)
- **boot F2 `settleRequiredPackageAdmission` unbounded**: `preparePromise`/`livePromise` were awaited bare after the gate had already timed out — exactly when the cook is judged too slow, embark parked until it *really* finishes, 'Still preparing' forever. Both now bounded by `REQUIRED_PACKAGE_SETTLE_TIMEOUT_MS = 45000` via `settleWithin` — a timeout publishes 'pending' like the gate instead of hanging the readiness promise.
- **boot F3 first-flight prefetch eligible-set**: `primeNextAuthoredAssetPlan`'s first-flight branch prefetched queued jobs whose entity had zero authored asset requests (ship-less/prop jobs held the slot for ~one serial admit, delaying the real ship warms). Eligible set now requires `entity.type === 'ship' || authoredUpgradeAssetRequests(job).length > 0`.

Applied at the asset/station/place trigger-wrap sites.
- **popin F4 poolSeal awaited without staleness re-check**: a cook superseded inside the `await poolSeal` gap resumed into post-seal bookkeeping on the dead sector. `cookStale()` re-check lands right after the await, before `jump.instancePoolSeal` is recorded.
- **boot F1 `admissionRunGeneration` only minted on game:new**: Continue never got a generation — `waitForOpeningGpuResources`' bareStamp read the previous run's sticky 'rejected' → early-false → throw → save unreachable. `beginLoadedGameTransition` now writes `state.render.admissionRunGeneration = token.generation`.
- **boot F3 `enterSerial` reset aliased pending tails**: `resetRunState` replaced `state.world`, so a surviving emit-slice tail replayed payloads whose `enterEpoch` compared against a re-minted serial — same-value aliasing let a stale replay mint into the new run. `state.enterSerialSeq` is now the state-root monotonic source: `resetRunState`/`enterSectorChunked` mint `enterSerial` from it so world-object replacement can never alias.
- **hitches F3 `_spawnTargetsPassKey` minted pre-loop**: the pass key stamped before the chunked pass ran — a superseded pass's `iterator.return()` left the key with a stale version so the next pass skipped its completion-version refresh. Key now stores only at completion (with the fresh `entityIndexVersion`); a superseded pass leaves no key and re-runs.
- **hitches F4 restore-lane epoch window**: pending emit tails could drain during restore's early yields while `enterSerial` still held the last live value — tail-drained replays minted into the dying world. `enterSerialSeq` now bumps before the `save:restoring` emit so every tail-drained replay mismatches and drops.
- **lod F2 `_holdExemptPersistentSkips` stamped `irrelevant`**: an `irrelevant` verdict is live-state and can flip mid-hold — stamping it skipped re-triage permanently for the window. Stamp now covers `alreadyServed` only.
- **lod F3 `FIRST_FLIGHT_SHIP_ADMISSION_RADIUS_WU` under-covered**: the fixed 700 WU left a non-closing band between the pin radius and the authored prefetch radius — now `authoredPrefetchRadius(tableTravelSpeed(live))` in both reads.
- **lod F4 deferred (contract-gated)**: `traffic._rng` shared-stream cook-vs-emit divergence cannot be fixed without re-ordering the emit-path draws — any dedicated mint stream changes the draw sequence → golden breaks. Documented as determinism-contract-gated, not implemented.
- **hitches F5 subsumed**: unstamped-provider re-derivation is covered by the FIFO design (covered systems drain once via their deferred entry; the provider pass after them is a dedupe-skip).

Verify: golden `47a` trajectory **bit-identical** (`8d4492dc…`, deterministic). Focused suites 162 green (sector/cook/arrival batch + system batch + heist/morrow/intervention/survivor/ace batch). ## W49 — 2026-10-03 (this commit; all 4 lanes)

Audit HEAD `c9e81a385` (post-merge `e61b60db1` W48 + modulepreload). All four lanes reported `saturated:false`. Saturation count: 0/3.

- **Double-invoke closed (convergent — lod F2, popin F5, hitches, boot)**: 15/16 defer sites pushed the *same* function object into the FIFO that was registered in `sectorCookProviders`, and the census concatenated both lists — the second call re-ran the materializer. The cooked-side dedupe (`_viaCook`) arms only on emit-stamps (`_emittedSector`/`_emittedEpoch`) the deferred path never mints, so a re-run was invisible to every consumer except traffic's, whose `_cleanup()` wipe+reminted the ambient roster. Census now filters the registered list by function identity against the drained FIFO (`deferredProviderSet`); a deferred system's provider never re-runs. Vesper's defer wraps a different closure (`() => _sync()`) — intentionally preserved as the ~free idempotent call.
- **lod F1 `sectorShellAdmission` unguarded `.finally`**: `compileSectorPipelines`'s `.finally` cleared the latch unconditionally — a superseded cook resolving under the live cook stripped it, so `cookLiveSceneGpu` skipped the entire arrival cohort (lazy per-frame compiles instead of the batch cook). Release now requires `this._liveSectorCookGeneration === cookGenerationAtAttach` (captured at listener attach).
- **lod F4 falsy-`exactSectorId` emits still deferred**: a `sector:enter` carrying no `sectorId`/`sector.id` deferred entries whose exactSectorId resolved `''` — the cook could never run for them (predicate had no sector gate), stranding the queue for the epoch filter. `sectorEnterCookWillRun` now mirrors the listener's sector-id clause.
- **hitches F2 + boot F2 stranded FIFO on skipped cook**: `prepareLiveSectorAfterJump`'s `'not-flight'`/`'no-sector-shell'` early-outs ran before the splice — a pause flip inside the ~4-5-frame emit-tail window left the whole cohort queued until a later enter's epoch filter dropped it (cohort never minted). New `drainDeferredEnterMaterializers(state, sector)` (core export, epoch-filtered live entries only) runs them inline at both early-outs.
- **popin F2 per-item staleness gap**: `cookStale()` was consulted per provider-iterator, not per atomic item — a continuous-membership flip mid-drive could mint into the superseded sector. Re-checked before every `iterator.next()`.
- **hitches F3 `startEmitSlice` unbounded predecessor flush**: `drainEmitSlice(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)` ran the whole tail unconditionally. Now bounded to one 4ms slice; a surviving tail queues the emit in `pendingSlicedEmits`, which `drainEmitSlice`'s completion path starts in order (listener-order preserved; `pendingEmitSliceCount` covers queued entries so saveSystem's restore wait converges).
- **hitches F4 refusal re-arm asleep while hidden**: paced re-arms relied on a 1s `setTimeout` — hidden-tab throttle parks it for minutes, boundary stays unarmed far past the TTL. Paced classes (`deferred-arena-dressing`, `invalid-upgrade-request`) now also listen for `visibilitychange` (fires instantly on un-hide) and arm when `monotonicNow() >= dueAt`. `cancelled-before-queue` + `regrade-evict-cooloff` arm immediately (detached boundary can't re-fire; cool-off is self-pacing).
- **popin F3 `invalid-upgrade-request` kept paced (deliberate)**: the lane suggested immediate arm; a still-on-glass boundary can re-refuse, and without pacing the trigger re-arms and re-refuses at 60Hz inside the hold. Accepted the rare extra 1s over the ping-pong.
- **Runtime fix (live verify, not a lane finding)**: `providerNow`/`providerYield`/`providerSliceStart` were block-scoped inside `if (Array.isArray(censusProviders))` but referenced by the departing-sector teardown after the block — ReferenceError on every deferred enter made teardown + the publication-guard tail dead code. Hoisted above the block.
- **Runtime fix (live verify)**: `coverBareMissionWrecks` was scoped to `liveSectorFullExtrasStubs`'s memo-else branch but referenced outside it — `jump:chargeStart` warm threw ReferenceError. Hoisted; `makeBareWreckCover` returns `cover.isCovered()` and the memo stores `bareCovered` so a memo-hit primes the flag instead of double-pushing residues.
- **lod F3 + hitches F5 witness-grid residual (documented, not fixed)**: a body that flips still→dynamic AND moves >1 cell inside a single tick can be filed under the stale cell — one-tick window, teleport-specific, bounded by the per-tick memo rebuild; live `pos` is still read at eval so only the unvisited-stale-cell miss exists. `lawWitnessPlanRowIsStill` + `rocks` key guard the common cases.
- **popin F4 deferred**: work ran before findings arrived (fixed items above instead).

Verify: golden `47a` trajectory **bit-identical** (`8d4492dc…`, deterministic — headless takes the inline path so the defer lane cannot move it by construction). Focused suites 102 green (event-bus contract 25/25 incl. new tail/queue cases, witness, save-envelope fidelity, ceres traffic cast, civilian variety, first-flight cook, admission budget, sector prewarm). In-browser forced-defer verify (gpu.software=false): Helios⇄Ceres jump drains FIFO 11→0/16→0, cohort mints (ships 12→22, wrecks 4→10), worst rAF gap 1.3s vs 3.8s un-deferred; no ReferenceErrors.

## W50 — 2026-10-03 (this commit; all 4 lanes)

Audit HEAD `0296ae639` (post-merge `c9e81a385` + `53a5ae7d5` W49 fixes pushed). All four lanes reported `saturated:false`. Saturation count: 0/3.

- **F1 — all four lanes convergent, W49 regression (critical)**: `cookGenerationAtAttach` was captured BEFORE `compileSectorPipelines(sector)` — the generation mint happens synchronously inside `prepareLiveSectorAfterJump` (:11880, before its first await), so `live !== attach` was always true after any real cook and `sectorShellAdmission` never released. On hardware GPU the first flight-mode non-continuous enter permanently froze `shouldFreezeFlightSubmit`/`shouldSkipFullTickSystems` — the sim died post-jump (SwiftShader CI blind: `gpu.software` early-outs before the mint). End-to-end reproduced by the forced-defer probe (277s freeze, 'busy' refusals).
- **F2 — all four lanes convergent, W49 regression**: `startEmitSlice`'s tail flush was `while (emitSlice) drainEmitSlice(...)` — exited only when the tail emptied, so `pendingSlicedEmits` could never fill (dead code) and a stacked sliced emit force-drained the entire predecessor tail inline (the W48 unbounded-brick regression returned). Now a single bounded 4ms pass engages the queue; plus a found-while-fixing stall edge — a queued emit whose event has zero live listeners dispatched and returned without starting the next queued entry (queue stranded). The `!fns` branch now dispatches then shift-starts the next entry.
- **boot F3 provider→teardown seam clock split**: the teardown loop minted its own fresh slice clock — last provider slice (≤8ms) + unsliced `collectFirstFlightCookEntities` (O(entityList)+asteroid sort) + first teardown slice (≤8ms) ran ~16ms+ contiguous between GPU yields. Teardown now rides `providerSliceStart`/`providerYield` and the entity collect sits behind a yield boundary.
- **lod F3 + popin F3 + lod F4 — defer-FIFO hygiene**: stale-epoch entries re-queued forever in `drainDeferredEnterMaterializers` (monotone serials → a mismatched epoch can never match again → permanent pinning + queue growth per early-out drain); dead entries now drop like the splice path's. A same-epoch re-emit stacked a duplicate of a system's own FIFO entry (both drain → double materialize); push is now deduped on (provider, epoch) returning true so the first entry still stands in. Three cook providers lacked the `world.currentSectorId` fallback (heistFacilities/recoveryEncounter/salvage — latent until a sector-omitting emitter exists); factionPresence was a lane false-positive (its `_onSectorEnterSteps` resolves the fallback at :568 itself).
- **Documented, not implemented**: hitches F3 early-out drain is deliberately atomic (the valve restoring pre-deferral emit behavior — slicing would strand mid-cohort under a live enter); hitches F4 vesper dual-drive is deliberate (unconditional deferred `_sync` keeps capture+clear on off-home enters; home-gated `_cookProvider` — code comment); lod F3-witness still→dynamic one-tick stale-cell (L impact; per-tick memo bounds it); boot F4 `liveEpoch==null` drain-bypass unreachable in production.
- **Regression verifications**: `deferredProviderSet` identity-skip verified against all 16 defer sites (vesper's anonymous-closure intent confirmed); `liveSectorGpuAdmission` copy retains spawn-sector idempotence; `gameRestarted` epoch guards hold; event-bus contract re-pinned to the bounded-flush contract (intermediate queued state + identical final ordering).

Verify: golden `47a` trajectory **bit-identical** (`8d4492dc…`, deterministic). Focused suites 31/31 event-bus contract green incl. re-pinned sliced-emit-behind-slice case. Forced-defer browser verify pending on the latch fix.

- **boot F1 `cookStale` blind to same-sector world replacement**: a restore into the same sector keeps `currentSectorId` identical while reissuing every entity — the cook's teardown resolved reissued ids against a dead world and `cookLiveSceneGpu` cooked the restored world with a stale `liveSectorFirstFlightIds`. `cookEnterSerial` is captured at the mint and consulted beside the generation and sector id.
- **boot F2 `pipelinePrecompileReady` unhandled rejection**: `.finally` propagates the cook's rejection — an unawaited stored promise escaped as an uncaught page error while residency kept drawing. `.catch(() => {})` at both stores (the loading arm's sibling already had it).
- **popin F1 + hitches F1 convergent — queued-emit restart exempt from the caller's deadline**: `drainEmitSlice`'s completion path called `startEmitSlice` → `drainEmitSlice(budget)` with `maxMs=Infinity`, so a ≤4ms pump call that finished a predecessor's tail also paid the queued emit's entire first slice (up to 32 `sector:enter` listeners) inline. `startEmitSlice` now threads the caller's remaining deadline into the forced flush AND the restart (epsilon floor keeps an expired window from widening back to Infinity; ≥1 listener still runs per drain call).
- **hitches F3 + popin F4 `bus.clear()` stranded `pendingSlicedEmits`**: `clear()` nulled `emitSlice` but left the queue — `pendingEmitSliceCount()` stayed >0 while `drainEmitSlice` early-returned forever (the queue only advances via `startEmitSlice`, unreachable with `emitSlice === null`) — saveSystem's restore wait could hang permanently. `clear()` now empties the queue.
- **popin F3 epochless deferred entries were drain-immortal**: a `null` epoch passed every drain's liveness check, so an entry pushed by an epochless synthetic emit would re-run its provider on whatever world was then current, forever. Push-time binding to `world.enterSerial` — the entry dies with the world that minted it.

- **hitches F5 salvage missing the `enterEpoch` guard**: 9 of 16 deferring systems early-return a stale-epoch payload; salvage's listener didn't — `_planForSector` trusted `payload.sectorId`, so a tail-sliced enter-A payload could overwrite live-B's plan and filter `state.salvage.points` to the dead sector (inline path only — deferral rejects before it). Now carries the sibling guard.

Sorts the cached pair instead.

Verify: golden `47a` trajectory **bit-identical** (`8d4492dc…`, deterministic). ## W53 — 2026-10-03 (all 4 lanes landed)

Audit HEAD `779b83fe7` (PR #215 tip). All four lanes reported `saturated: false`. Saturation count: 0/3.

The unifying theme: the **queued-emit interleave class** — W52's cap-overflow force-drain can deliver a stale enter tail inside a newer enter's live window, so every consumer of `sector:enter` needed to tell its own emit's epoch from a superseded one.

- **popin F3 + lod F1 + boot F5 emit-arm epoch stamp**: `sectorShellAdmission` armed at emit time (W52) but carried no identity — a stale-tail delivery, keep-GPU flip, or a synchronous throw between the arm and the listener's `.finally` registration could not distinguish this emit's latch from a newer enter's. The arm now stamps `sectorShellAdmissionSerial = world.enterSerial`; releases compare the serial so an older emit's `.finally` can't unlatch a newer enter's arm and a missed release can't pin the shell forever.
- **popin F2 unguarded enter listeners**: `encounterDirector._onSectorEnter` and `livingPoiBehaviors._activateSector` had no `enterEpoch` guard — a replayed tail reseeded pressure, wiped pending, and flipped `activeSectorId`/`activeByZone` to the departed sector. Both now reject `enterEpoch !== world.enterSerial` (epochless synthetic payloads still run — tests and the census cook mint none). The `_pump` due-scan also gained a `it.sectorId !== _currentSectorId()` skip: beats stamped by a pre-guard tail can never fire on the live world.
- **popin F5 + hitches-related deferred-FIFO clock**: durable schedulers invoked inside a deferred provider read `state.simTime` — wobbled by however many frames the FIFO sat. Each entry now mints `clock = payload.enterSimTime` at defer time; both drains (inline `drainDeferredEnterMaterializers` and sliced `drainDeferredEnterSlice`, plus the cook's splice map via `deferredProviderClock`) pin it on `render._deferredEnterClock` around every `provider()`/`iterator.next()` with `finally` clear — deferred delivery stamps the same dueAt the emit path did.
- **popin F4 authored/decode picks lacked sector scope**: `kickAuthoredBoundaryUpgrade`, `pickDecodeRunwayCandidates`, and `kickSpawnedEntityDecode` graded entities whose `homeSectorId` had already departed. New shared `entityHomeSectorMismatch(entity, state)` early-outs at all three.
- **popin F5 aceMemory `nowOf` wobble**: `dueAt` schedules (`_scheduleCultureIntro`, `_schedulePlanetChallenges`) read the drain-time clock. `nowOf` now prefers `payload.enterSimTime → payload.t → render._deferredEnterClock → state.simTime`, and both sites pass the payload.
- **asteroidSites pre-guard mutation**: `this._repairSweepWanted = true` armed before the staleEnter check — the only pre-guard mutation among the guarded siblings; a stale payload now mutates nothing.
- **lod F2 collect seam sliced**: `prepareLiveSectorAfterJump`'s `collectMeshPresentationEntities` ran ~5–12ms atomic inside the cook. New chunked twins in `presentationSources` (`collectJournalPresentationEntitiesChunked` + `appendNearbyLedgerRowsChunked` behind a scalar-snapshotted `_nearbyLedgerRowsContext` — module scratch could be rewritten mid-yield) ride the census slice clock on a LOCAL `presentation` array (the shared `_presentationMeshScratch` could be emptied by a `kickDecodeRunwayAssets` beat mid-collect — a silent row-drop hazard found in review).
- **lod F3 mid-cook promotions dropped `irrelevant`**: entities promoted during the census (deferred providers minting bodies the pre-collect list never saw) got `irrelevant` stamps and missed the reveal. A post-census newcomer sweep re-runs admission on rows minted after the collect snapshot.
- **hitches F1 continuous-seam FIFO owner**: a `continuous:true` noTeleport enter collapsed `enterSectorChunked` into one sim tick, so deferred FIFO entries drained by the old inline path paid the whole queue inside the seam. `sectorEnterSeamDeferWillRun` (flight + sector + continuous + non-software GPU) routes them through the FIFO instead; the handoff hold's exempt beat drives `drainDeferredEnterSlice(4ms)` per entry-iterator, and the hold end + hard-enter clear drain the rest (dead-epoch entries drop first — same supersession semantics).
- **hitches F2 `drainEmitSlice` count**: fixed listener count scaled `ceil(pendingEmitSliceCount()/4)` so a queued tail can't dominate the window.
- **boot F1 `prepareLiveSectorBeforeFlight` had zero staleness checks**: ~20–120s of awaits with no epoch guard — a mid-cook enter cooked the departing sector and stamped `_sessionLiveSectorCookedId` on the wrong world. `_liveSectorCookGeneration` + `cookEnterSerial` + `sectorId` captured at the mint; 18 `cookStale()` checks before every blocking await, the cooked-id stamp, and a generation-guarded `finally`.
- **boot F2 `_sessionRecookKeepGpu` recompute in listener**: the keep-GPU decision re-derived inside the `mode:changed` listener could disagree with the mint's; now read from the record the mint wrote.
- **boot F3 reconcile evict inline dispose**: the residency-evict path ran `disposeObject` per row inside the presented frame. Now routes through `_despawnDisposeQueue` (the kill-burst path's bounded drain).
- **boot F4 context-restore rebuild had no world-swap guard**: `_rebuildRestoredGpuResources` resolved seconds of awaits then stamped `pipelinePrecompileReady` unconditionally — an enter during the rebuild declared pipelines ready for a world it never linked. `restoreEnterSerial` captured at the mint; the stamp skips when the serial moved or `_contextLost` re-armed (the newest enter/restore owns the stamp).
- **Micro-nits**: post-cap `continue`→`break` in `collectFirstFlightCookEntities` and the renderer's `rockHits` loop (the cap check re-evaluated per remaining row after exhaustion); `has(key)` continue semantics preserved.

## W54 — 2026-10-03 (all 4 lanes landed)

Audit HEAD `78f654cdd` (PR #215 tip). All four lanes reported `saturated: false`. Saturation count: 0/3.

The unifying theme: the **deferred-enter liveness window** — three independent lane findings traced to the same question: when exactly does the outgoing enter's epoch die, and which drain owns the cohort minted under it?

- **lod F1 + hitches F1 (convergent) drop-only hard-enter clear**: the hard-enter tail ran `drainDeferredEnterMaterializers(state, _sectorHandoffSector)` — inline, inside the emit, against the DEPARTED sector — executing the whole live-epoch cohort that ~17 earlier PRODUCTION_INIT_ORDER listeners had just pushed, and making the census splice dead code (both lanes verified it never fires in production). New `dropDeadDeferredEnterEntries(state)` sweeps only dead-epoch entries; the fresh cohort stays queued for the census splice, which drives it under the slice clock against the correct sector.
- **lod F2 `deferredEnterNow`/`deferredEnterTick`**: W53 pinned `_deferredEnterClock` around provider drives but provider-side stamp sites still read raw `state.simTime`/`state.tick`. Two helpers prefer the pinned clock/tick; adopted at traffic (`_rebaseCeresCausalPhaseEnds`, `recordCreatedTick`), survivorPod `startedAt`, missions `acceptedAt_s`/`deadline_s`, factionPresence `recordCreatedTick`, uniqueWrecks `simTime` + `_fire`, intervention `t`. `enterTick` minted on the enterPayload so FIFO entries carry the emit's exact tick.
- **lod F3 `iterator.return()` on drops**: dropped dead-epoch chunked-provider entries now call `iterator.return?.()` (both drains + splice-drop) so generator locals die with the drop instead of living to GC.
- **popin lane (`32d809544`)**: `enterEpoch` guards at routeFollower/automation/chronicler listeners, latched `homeSector` scope on deferred picks, asteroidSites arm gate.
- **hitches F2 shared-scratch snapshots**: `appendNearbyLedgerRowsChunked` snapshots `_meshRockScratch`/`_meshFarScratch` at creation — reconcile collects could refill the shared module scratches mid-walk and silently swap the iterated rows.
- **hitches F3 `_seamDeferredDrain` post-hold flag**: hold-end used to inline-drain the remaining FIFO inside the hold-release frame; now arms the flag and a post-hold block drives `drainDeferredEnterSlice(4)`/frame until empty with `_sectorHandoffSector` still pinned. Hard-enter clears the flag — a lingering seam driver must not step the new epoch's entries against the departed seam sector.
- **hitches F4 early serial mint**: `enterSerial` bumped right after the `sector:exit` emit (was minted ~90 lines later at the payload tail). The gap left dead-epoch FIFO entries live while `enter-pre-emit` yields let hold beats step them into the departing sector. Same-sector enters still mint at the payload site.
- **boot F1 receipt guards**: the survival depth-sweep stamp, receipt recapture, and `parkWarmRoots` are gated on `!cookStale()` — a superseded cook could stamp depth keys onto the new world and sweep its live roots.
- **boot F2 `liveSectorFirstFlightIds` staleness**: a superseded cook's late id-stamp could land after the live cook's own, and the departing sweep would then dispose live-mounted meshes under the new world. Stamps generation-gated in both cook functions; `mode:changed → loading` clears the id set + `_arrivalRosterIds` on both branches.
- **boot F3 `stale` predicate threading**: `waitForAuthoredUpgradeQueueIdle`/`waitForOpeningCompositionSettled` accept `options.stale` and bail `{superseded:true}`/`{reason:'superseded'}`; threaded at all 5 wait-helper call sites; `cookOverBudget` includes `cookStale()`; seal loop and `drainMeshBuildsBehindShell` bail pre-step.
- **boot F4 bounded waits**: `waitForCaptured`/`waitForPending` (pipeline + GPU-residency trackers) accept `{timeoutMs, stale}`; the post-opening captured drain is bounded at 15s so a never-settling compile can't park the drain past the 20s startup gate.

Verify: golden `47a` trajectory **bit-identical** (`8d4492dc…`, deterministic). Focused suites green (opening-admission 27/27, fb-corridor-sector-enter 3/3, crucible-ten-wave-shell 11/11, opening-mesh-defer 13/13, render-target-pipeline-warmup wait paths). - **popin F2 seam veil extension**: a deferred cohort that outlived the 1.5 s blend kept mounting entities post-veil onto presented frames. Hold-end now re-arms `_sectorHandoffStreamHoldS` in beats while the FIFO is non-empty — the tail rides the deferred streaming window and the same beat-driven drain — escaped by a hard 2 s extension budget, then the W54 post-hold slice drain takes over. The wave closed the deferred-clock/pin system's last structural seams: **a foreign-epoch queue head could pin the post-hold driver forever** (polling a queue it was gated from touching, ~O(queue)/frame), and **the splice pin leaked across `providerYield()`** (a yielded frame exposed live-path pin-helper readers to the frozen emit clock).

- **lod F1 missed stamp sites**: 4 more raw `state.simTime`/`state.tick` reads reachable inside deferred providers pinned — traffic `_newPassengerLinerItinerary` simTime + `_ensureCeresCausalChain`/`_emitCeresCausalReceipt` pins, missions `_epoch`/`_dayKey`/`_syncWaypointRoute`/`_syncCampaignSidecarAfterAdvance`/`clockOut` pins, wingmen `startedTick`/`issuedTick`, factionPresence `startedTick`, uniqueWrecks `_scheduleSeededTimers`/`_pumpComplications`/`_requestEncounter`, traffic `_salvorNoticeReady` x3 + `_listSalvageTargets` + `_priorityCourierStatus`. Now `return()` fires unconditionally before `live` is returned — providers always re-run fresh from the splice.
- **lod F3 live-foreign-head early clear**: post-hold `_seamDeferredDrain` polled the FIFO every 4ms even when the head belonged to a NEWER enter's epoch (the epoch gate could only `break` — flag stayed armed forever). The driver now peeks `rest[0]`; a head stamped with the live `world.enterSerial` (≠ its hold epoch) clears flag+sector+epoch immediately — the newer enter's own owner drives that queue (its hard-enter/hold sites already do).
- **popin F1 missed pin sites**: ~14 additional deferred clock reads pinned alongside the lod batch (same scoping rule).
- **popin F2 splice pin scoping**: the W55 splice pin wrapped the whole provider drive — including `await providerYield()` — so a yielded frame left the frozen emit clock live for live-path readers (liner re-legs, mission accepts, `recordBeatStep`). New `pinDeferredEnter`/`unpinDeferredEnter` closures restore the previous pin before each yield and re-pin in `finally` — the pin now covers ONLY synchronous provider code.
- **boot F1 opening-cook provider parity**: the opening path drove all 14 `sectorCookProviders` in one atomic burst — a throw skipped the entire cook tail (entity census, leftover teardown, seals, receipt) and a fat cohort froze the loading presenter. Now mirrors the jump census: per-provider try/catch with ledger failure records (`opening.sectorCookProvider`), chunked-iterator drive, 8ms `yieldLiveSectorGpu` slicing, `cookStale` bail.
- **boot F2 aftermathWrecks pins**: `registerWreckFieldSource`/`_registerAftermathFields`/`_populateField` stamped raw `state.simTime` inside the deferred tree — now `deferredEnterNow` (the last provider file lacking the import).
- **boot F3 deadline-threaded batch.drain**: `admitOpeningUnitsAcrossSlices` called `batch.drain()` bare — a wedged link honored the 20s drain default instead of the caller's `materialSettle` deadline. Now `{timeoutMs: deadlineMs - elapsed}`.
- **boot F4 sliced leftover teardown**: the opening leftover-mesh sweep (`_meshes` dispose burst, run before recook/F9 collects) got the same 8ms yield + `cookStale` slicing its jump twin carries.
- **boot F5 bounded behind-shell flush**: `flushPipelinesBehindShell` called `drainPendingPipelineAdmissions()` bare — a wedged captured plan could park the `liveSectorPipelineFlush` dedupe for the session. Now `{timeoutMs: 8000}`.

**W61** (all four lanes `saturated: false` — streak 0/3; landed on `devin/1791064509-perf-w60` at merged tip `46b481c42`):

- **lod F2 + boot F1 + hitches F1 (three-lane convergence) — `_postOpeningRescanRequested` orphaned at the pass-cap exit**: a join landing during pass 3's awaits armed the flag with no consumer left, and `released` still stamped — the joiner's cohort compiled cold at first draw. `released` now requires `!_postOpeningRescanRequested`; the flag clears at all four reset sites (the armed flag survives epoch advance to be consumed by the next full pass); the admit leg exits cheap when the rescan unit set is empty (`rescanUnits` precomputed once per rescan, was re-derived per leg).
- **hitches F2 + boot F3 — converged-cycle break + honest work counts**: `enqueueHoldExemptMeshBuildsSliced` reminted the ~5-12 ms collect until `sliceMs` expired even when the world stopped changing — a completed cycle that queued nothing and started no decode now breaks instead of re-paying the spatial refill + journal walk for the hold's duration. `enqueueMeshBuildCandidate` returns bool and both commit iterators return their own push/start counts — the verdict is the iterator's own work, not a queue-length delta foreign drains could falsify.
- **Verify-fix — commit starvation under a spent collect clock**: the sliced commit (W60 hitches F4) shared the invocation's `started` clock with the collect — when the collect spent the whole 4 ms, the enqueue/kick pumps exited after ~zero steps, and in a heavy-sector hold the commit could starve indefinitely (surfaced as the decode-runway wreck test flapping). The commit now mints its own per-invocation clock; commits of ≤16 rows drain inline, matching the pre-sliced completing-beat contract for small exempt sets.
- **lod F1 — warm-class eviction was LRU-by-release**: `sortSoftEvictionCandidates` drained the earliest-`lastReleaseAtMs` warm first, so a file whose decode restarted (fresh claim) evicted before a genuinely stale cohort-mate. Owners stamp `lastWarmClaimAtMs` at mint, on decodeWarm→decodeServed upgrade retain, and on every new-owner retain; the warm class sorts oldest-claim-first.
- **lod F3 + popin F1 — crucible warm coverage bookkeeping**: `warm.coveredEnemyIds` mints from `launchEligibility` at mark time; the catch path unmarks each covered id from `_swarmWarmCoveredEnemyIds`; the three warm-owned `requestAuthoredUpgrade` calls (spawnable, ship kick, hulk — not the live-entity boundary-kick loop) pass `isResidencyOwnerActive: () => warm.building === true` so an in-flight warm's owner reads active.

- **lod F4 — `authoredUpgradePlan` re-resolved per kick**: ships walked `authoredPreloadPlanForEntity` and non-ships `placeFileForEntity` on every runway kick — memoized per job via a WeakMap.
- **lod F5 — `kickBoundaryUpgrade` re-derived time-to-glass**: it now takes `runwayEnv.secondsFor`, reusing the pick's per-entity memo (`entityTimeToGlassSeconds` with identical args) — other callers keep the direct derivation.
- **popin F3 — mid-walk aliveness recheck**: the kick's start loop re-verifies `entity.alive !== false` before claiming a budgeted start slot (the evaluate ran before a yield; the row could die mid-pick).
- **popin F3b / hitches F5 — dead sync `kickDecodeRunwayAssets` deleted**: every caller had moved to the chunked twin.
- **lod F6 — chunked/sync pick parity pinned**: `pickDecodeRunwayCandidatesSteps` driven through `drainSteps` + a `stepsStarts` mirror gets two new tests (800 randomized lists identical to the sync pick; cap + supersede claims parity); `decodeRunwayPick.js` `_key` documented write-then-read-within-one-segment.

- **boot F2 — Continue had no physics-prep bound**: `src/main.js` wraps `continuePhysicsPrep` in `startGatePulse('physics-authority', 0.94, …)` + a 20 s `Promise.race` to `GameStartReadinessError('PHYSICS_BACKEND_UNAVAILABLE', 'physics-authority', …)` with `stopPhysicsPulse` in finally — parity with the fresh-game contract.

### W62 — coverage unmark, stall-abort union, commit slice floor, parked-kick refresh, depth-readiness gate, honest depth marks, bounded physics gate (b2fd0e890 + ef74c7494)

All four lanes reported, all `saturated: false`.

- **lod F1 / popin F1 — crucible begin-warm coverage never unmarked**: the catch path gated `warm.coveredEnemyIds` with `Array.isArray`, but the mint is a `Set` (swarmEligibleEnemyIds) — a failed begin pinned swarm-warm coverage for the rest of the run. Now iterates the Set and deletes each id.
- **lod F2 — stall-abort plan re-derive**: `abortStalledUpgradeJob` now unions the mint-stamped `job.assetUrls` with a freshly re-derived `authoredUpgradePlan(job)` (memo dropped first) — ship decodes re-derive the plan at task start and entity identity can mutate mid-flight, so plan-only drops could leave wedged authored tasks behind.
- **lod F3 — completing-beat clock stacking**: the hold-exempt commit ran on a full second `sliceMs` clock after the collect's — completing beats measured ~8-16 ms vs the ~4 ms bound. Commit now gets `sliceMs − collectRemainder` plus a 2 ms floor (preserves the W61 starvation fix without a second whole slice; `commitSliceMs`).
- **lod F5 — parked kick graded on mint-time snapshot**: `kickDecodeRunwayAssetsSteps` minted env/seconds/runway ctx at generator start; a pick suspended across beats graded crossing rows on stale terms. The driver bumps `_holdExemptBeatStamp` once per invocation and the kick refreshes its snapshot on any resume under a newer stamp (manual `yield*` delegation so the resume boundary is observable).
- **popin F3 — deferred upgrade post skipped relevance re-check**: the `yieldAfterPresent` post re-derived glass terms live but never re-ran `canRequestAuthoredUpgrade` — an entity killed or sector-pinned during the yield still posted serial-lane decode+compose. Live re-grade added.
- **popin F2 — rescan depth leg re-staged every non-empty delta**: `compileShadowDepthPipelines` ran its whole-scene ceremony per join-trickle pass. New per-renderer depth-variant signature gate (`collectUnstagedShadowCasters`: material uuid × mesh kind × live light/fog census, marked by every successful stage); repeat passes over the same set skip the leg entirely.
- **lod F4/F6 — smalls**: write-only `_holdExemptEnqueueBefore` deleted; the Continue physics gate attaches a rejected bring-up's real reason as `error.cause` instead of folding it into the 20 s timeout path.
- **Test re-pin**: `encounter-pending-decode-warm` adjacency pin now tolerates the W58 sliced-poll `yield` between `updatePredictedSectorPrewarm` and `warmEncounterPendingDecode` (deliberate contract from ab294cb59).
- **boot F1 — leaked loading-gate pulse**: `startLoadingGatePulse`'s emit loop never observed supersession — a pulse orphaned by a transition replace woke every 500 ms forever. The emit now self-clears (`clearInterval` on `!isCurrent`); `try/finally` around `waitForAuthoredPartLibrary` and `waitForInitialAuthoredVisualsWithRetry` so a failed start always stops its pulse.
- **boot F2 — swarm coverage was a Set of flags, not claims**: a failed begin's catch cleared ids another in-flight warm also stamped. `_swarmWarmCoveredEnemyIds` is now a Map ref-count — mint increments, catch/unmark decrements, deferred-lane instanceof/mint/mark sites updated. (Pinned: 2 `swarm-deferred-warm` source pins re-pinned to the ref-count mint.)
- **boot F3 — New Game `waitForPhysics` unbounded**: mirror of the W62-lod Continue fix — `physicsPrep` now races a 20 s timer; a wedge anywhere inside `prepareBackend` fails to `PHYSICS_BACKEND_UNAVAILABLE` instead of holding the shell forever.
- **boot F4 — evaluated, skipped**: SG-02 owner after a raced Continue start is benign (self-superseding, non-compounding) per the lane's own optional flag.
- **hitches F1 — depth-staged signatures over-marked**: `markCastersDepthStaged` recorded every collected caster, including ones outside the shadow camera's ortho that never drew — certified readiness never proved, so `collectUnstagedShadowCasters` never re-collected them and their depth variant linked inside a presented frame on ortho entry. Marks now require the wrapped `renderBufferDirect` to have observed the caster's depth draw (under-marking bias: a false negative only re-runs the cheap rescan).
- **hitches F2 — off-clock ledger refill inside the hold beat**: `started` minted before the first `warmNearbyLedgerRows` refill so the spatial query debits this beat's slice, plus an elapsed recheck between the remint's refill and the iterator mint — a flip-aligned handoff beat no longer stacks refill+collect+commit+refill+drain into ~12-15 ms.
- **hitches F3 — commit-phase `.next()`s lacked the try/abandon guard**: enqueue and kick pumps get the collect pump's abandon path — a throwing step no longer parks a dead generator on the owner to be misread as done next beat.

### W63 — depth-drawability signatures, promotion cast-flag withhold, tolerate-miss remint, stale physics tail, warm coverage unmark lattice, stale physicsPrep re-validation, covered-disc first mint (all 4 lanes)

All four lanes reported `saturated: false`.

- **lod F1 — depth stage collected never-draw classes + ungated main leg**: `collectPotentialShadowCastSubjects` admitted casters that could never draw in the pass — all-materials-invisible meshes, geometry-null stubs (the revealer pushes `visible=false` on those), and layer-mismatched roots — permanently unstaged entries re-collected under every delta, and the main leg re-paid the whole-set staging per join re-arm with no signature gate. Collect now excludes geometry-null drawables and invisible-material meshes; `casterDepthSignatures` skips invisible materials and keys `${material.uuid}|kind|morph|custom|ly:${layersMask}|variant|lightSig`; `markCastersDepthStaged` marks an undrawn caster only when its undrawable class is signature-expressible (layer mismatch vs the stage camera — the pass tests `object.layers` against the live camera's layers); the post-opening main leg runs `collectUnstagedShadowCasters` and stages only the unstaged set; the rescan leg narrows its ceremony to the unstaged list.
- **lod F2 (H) — promotion cold-link**: `allowRealtimeShadowCast` flips `castShadow` on at ortho entry, but post-release joiners short-circuited 'already-released' and `rescanDelta` covers new roots only — a promoted root's depth variant linked inside the next presented shadow refresh (~460 ms class). New `_syncShadowCasterPolicyChecked` wraps all five policy-sync sites: a cast-band promotion queues a deferred micro `compileShadowDepthPipelines` via `yieldAfterPresent` (coalesced per Set, filtered by `collectUnstagedShadowCasters`, `SF_ShadowPromoteDepthAdmission`).
- **lod F3 — remint refill still atomic**: `warmNearbyLedgerRows`'s spatial query ran unbounded inside the remint leg on a memo-miss. `_nearbyLedgerRowsContext`/`warmNearbyLedgerRows`/`collectMeshPresentationEntitiesChunked` take `opts.tolerateMiss` — a flipped spatial key rides the previous disc's scratch (subset coverage; per-row live-origin filters keep verdicts correct) and the next beat's mint refills under a fresh clock.
- **popin F1 (H) — stale `physicsPrep` settle tail**: after the outer 20 s timeout → retry, init1's continuation still ran `_updateSg02DynamicAuthority(0, state)` + `_publishRuntime(state)` against run 2's live owner — an out-of-schedule `_sg02.step` plus an early drain of run-2 contact receipts (sim-determinism risk). `prepareSg02DynamicAuthority` snapshots `_sg02Token` before the init await and returns false when it no longer owns the authority.
- **popin F2 — deferred-warm `unmark` on the live field**: `run:ended` nulls `_swarmWarmCoveredEnemyIds`; a late async rejection threw TypeError mid-catch (skipping the settle `.then` → `warm.building` stuck → pinned residency leases) and, worse, could decrement a *new* run's rows. The Map instance is captured at mark time (`coveredMap`) and `unmark`/`unmarkEnemy` operate on it.
- **popin F3 — deferred rejection never tore down the root**: sync throw detached + spliced `_rosterPrewarmRoots`; async rejection only unmarked — half-built roots stacked under the same name per retry. Shared `teardown()` closure now runs on both catch paths.
- **popin F4 — per-spec build failures stayed coverage-claimed**: a failed exemplar claimed its archetype for the whole run, so the first live spawn paid the in-round compose+link the warm exists to kill. Build catches (ship + hulk, launch + deferred) decrement the spec's `lootTableId`/`hulkVisual.lootTableId` row — idempotent per warm (`failedCoverageMarks`/`unmarked`) so a shared hulk file can't double-decrement.
- **popin F5 — depth-signature param-mutation hole sealed**: `getDepthMaterial` clones variants on `alphaTest`-gated map/alphaMap, displacementMap, alphaToCoverage, clipShadows, and per-call side/shadowSide — none keyed by `material.uuid`. The signature tuple now encodes those discriminants; a post-stage flip mints a fresh unstaged signature and re-collects. (Latent today — no live flip path; sealed against future cloak/damage/attach mutations.)
- **popin F6 — covered by lod F2** (the same promotion hook re-stages in-place castability flips).
- **lod secondary — `_discardEarlyCrucibleWarm` dropped covered rows without decrementing**: a discarded begin-warm left its archetype claims minted for the whole run, so a retried begin / the deferred lane skipped re-warming them. The discard now decrements its `coveredEnemyIds` rows on the live map.
- **boot F1 (H) — stale `earlyContinuePhysicsPrep` verdict adopted verbatim**: a spec-armed prep minted at `save:envelopeSpec*` stayed latched after settling; a New Game attempt between kick and Continue bumps `_sg02Token` and disposes the authority it resolved against — the prep resolves `false` (post-W63-token-guard, deterministically) or `true` for a dead owner — and the gate adopted the stale verdict verbatim: spurious `PHYSICS_BACKEND_UNAVAILABLE` bounce (mode A) or entry with a null `_sg02` → first-tick remint + D26 freeze window (mode B). Adoption now re-validates: `continuePhysicsPrep` always chains a fresh non-reset `prepareBackend` (idempotent, joins `_sg02Init` mid-settle, re-mints only when torn down — never `reset:true` mid-restore); a non-`true` settle self-clears the latch so later envelope kicks re-mint.
- **hitches F1 (M) — promotion stage outraced by its own dirty flag**: `_queueShadowDepthStage` resolves ≥ frame N+1 (yieldAfterPresent → background task), but a band promotion applies `castShadow` in-frame N and the pose re-mint marks the map dirty — the same frame's refresh drew the unstaged variant live, so the stage only helped casters off-glass at promotion (the common visible-in-ortho entry still cold-linked). The wrapper now withholds `castShadow` on band-0→1 promotions with unstaged signatures (`syncOpts.allowCast=false` — band/pose/dirty untouched), queues `{root,lodLevel,entity}` in a Map, and the stage's `.then` re-syncs the real policy on every queued root — staged roots enter the cast band on already-linked programs, and a skipped/failed stage restores flags rather than leaving casters permanently shadowless. Band gate (`shadowCasterBand(root) !== 1`) keeps the signature walk off the same-band per-frame path.
- **hitches F2 — outer-catch unmark ignored `failedCoverageMarks`**: a per-spec failure decremented + marked, then a later throw in the unguarded segment decremented every stamped id again — a co-claiming warm's row dropped 2→1→0 (safe direction: duplicate re-warm, not skip). Both whole-warm decrement loops (begin catch + `_discardEarlyCrucibleWarm`) now skip ids in `failedCoverageMarks`.
- **hitches F3 — beat bound aspirational, now enforced**: (a) the first-mint refill got `tolerateMiss: 'covered'` — a strict mode added beside the remint's loose `true`: the stamped disc must version-match AND geometrically contain the walk disc (true superset → identical rows), and a new `hasLiveDisc` gate (same state + same field/far objects) seals the empty-scratch starvation hole the loose mode had on first-ever use; (b) the commit's guaranteed floor now only applies while the collect stayed inside its slice — an overspent collect leaves the floor at 0 so the beat can't stack collect+refill+forced-commit past the bound (parked iterators resume next beat).
- **hitches F4 — evaluated, skipped**: kick-iterator re-walk after an abandon re-pays warm prefixes for rows already kicked — but every kick is idempotent (`pending` Set, `authoredUpgradePromise`, `_decodeRunwayPackagedFiles` fkey), so correctness holds and a kickedIds stamp would skip legitimate re-grades after env refresh.
