### W64 — packaged-commit shadow re-sync, OFF→ON depth stage, loose-tolerance dead-disc gate (lanes reporting)

- **popin F1 (M) — packaged-body commits never re-ran the shadow-caster traverse**: `attachPackagedBody`/`attachPackagedScenarioProp` mount `packaged` inside an async continuation; the children keep three.js `castShadow=false` until a band/LOD flip re-traverses the root — station/place authored bodies, hulks, drones, pods stayed permanently shadowless when the root already held castBand 1, and the eventual flip drew their never-staged depth signatures in-frame. New render port `syncPackagedBodyShadowPolicy` (capturePort'd into `residencyOptionsForBoundary` beside `touchAuthoredExactTarget`) invalidates + runs `_syncShadowCasterPolicyChecked` at both commit sites — the withhold/stage machinery owns any promotion the swap introduces.
- **popin F2 (M) — shadows OFF→ON drew never-staged depth signatures**: band promotions applied `castShadow` while the map was off, but both the withhold gate and `_queueShadowDepthStage` require `_shadowSettingOn` — the ON transition pinned `shadowMap.enabled` and the first presented refresh linked every accumulated unstaged signature in-frame. The settings handler now runs `_stageShadowDepthOnSettingEnable` between `_ensureKeyLightShadows` and `_syncShadowMapEnabled`: a scene-wide `collectUnstagedShadowCasters` filter + `compileShadowDepthPipelines` (`SF_ShadowEnableDepthAdmission`, forceEnable) inside the menu handler.
- **popin F3 — loose-tolerance remint paid an unbounded refill on a dead disc**: `tolerateMiss:true` required `hasLiveDisc`, so a mid-hold `asteroidField`/`farActors` swap (save restore, field rebuild, sector jump under a live hold) made the remint run the full grid query inside the beat remainder — debit-bounded, not slice-bounded. The remint now serves an empty row set for that beat (dead-field scratches cleared) — the next clean-start mint refills under the strict `'covered'` gate on a fresh clock; per-row filters plus the commit evaluator guard the interim.
- **popin F4 — evaluated, deferred**: `'covered'` ride cadence is intended (refill only on cell-flip/version-bump beats, debited); walk-midpoint cell-boundary hover adds at most the pre-'covered' cadence — a walk-disc hysteresis would under-collect unless it preserves containment. Accepted per the lane's own L/medium rating.
- **popin F5 — latent note**: band-1 roots whose material signature mutates without `invalidateShadowCasterPolicy` stay unstaged forever; no live mutation path exists on casting roots today — future material-swap paths must route through invalidation.
- **lod F1 (H) — depth signatures stamped under the stripped light census**: `markCastersDepthStaged` computed `lightCensusSignature(lightingScene)` at mark time — after every non-key scene light was reparented into staging — so marks recorded `l1|f1` while every query reads the live `l12|f1`: `staged.has()` could never hit, the band-0 withhold fired on every promotion (each one showed a shadowless presented frame then paid the full reparent+census+depth ceremony), the post-opening depth legs re-staged the whole castable set per pass, and `_stagedDepthSignatures` grew dead `l1|f1` entries. The census is now taken before the reparent loop and threaded into the mark; the test rig gained a second permanently-mounted light so the mark/query census split is exercised (verified: new dedup test fails pre-fix, passes post-fix).
- **lod F2 — already landed by popin F2** (`_stageShadowDepthOnSettingEnable` is the OFF→ON scene-wide stage the lane asked for).
- **lod F3 — evaluated, deferred (L/M)**: band-1 dirty-resync material swaps mint a new depth signature but the queued stage resolves one present late — a same-frame `needsUpdate` refresh could draw the unstaged variant first. Narrow window (reskin-on-caster + refresh lands in the gap); the rock reskin lane already stages its own instance synchronously. Accepting the residual per the lane's risk rating — a synchronous single-root stage inside the dirty-sync path is the documented future fix.
- **hitches F1 (M–H) — promote `.then` was one unbounded task**: a 50-caster burst paid ~3 traverses + census + two 2048² shadow renders + links + N restores in a single background task that delayed the next rAF by its whole cost. The queue now drains ≤8 roots per arm, re-arming per present while pending remains — marks cover only observed draws so incremental staging is legal; unqueued roots keep their withheld cast flag.
- **hitches F2 (M) — per-root whole-scene census inside presented frames**: the withhold gate recomputed `lightCensusSignature` per withheld root per frame inside `syncEntityViews` — the W63 in-frame tax, scaling with ortho re-entry. Memoized per entity-view pass (`_viewSyncSeq`) and threaded into `collectUnstagedShadowCasters`' new `lightSigOverride`.
- **hitches F3 (M) — 'covered' could never ride a cell flip**: stamped and needed radii were identical, so any quantized-cell flip of the union origin broke containment (`dist ≤ 0` impossible) and a rim-hovering/cruising player paid the unsliced `queryAsteroidField`+`queryFarActors` refill every ~100ms beat. The minted disc now collects one cell-diagonal (312 WU) beyond the needed radius — a paid-for superset so the flip rides; `meshSpatialKeyMatches` keeps the unpadded `needRadius` for the exact path; `far.collectDisc` claims only collected coverage.
- **hitches F4 — noted only**: `_stageShadowDepthOnSettingEnable` stages the whole scene synchronously in a settings emit — user-initiated/rare, deliberate.
- **boot F1 (L) — skipped re-kick serialized inside the gate**: an envelope emit landing while a doomed prep pended returned early on the held latch and never re-minted until the next emit/finalize. `kickPending` self-reschedules the kick on settle-clear (guards re-run: mode + cold-owner).
- **boot F2 (L) — failed `RAPIER.init` re-paid per mint**: the catch reset `rapierRuntimePromise=null`, so a doomed env re-ran the whole WASM bootstrap on every per-tick re-prepare forever. ≥2 consecutive failures now refuse with a 15s→120s backing-off window; success resets.

### W65 — adaptive depth-stage arms, impact ordering, per-mesh in-band withhold, publication-release pacing, despawn-queue rebuild corpse, rapier backoff guard across both lanes (lod + popin + boot lanes; hitches in flight)

All reporting lanes returned `saturated: false`.

- **lod F1 — fixed 8-root arm slices re-paid the arm ceremony per batch**: a large promotion burst drained 8 roots per arm, each arm repaying the reparent+camera+depth-render+restore ceremony with identical opts. The slice cap is now adaptive — `ceil(n/2)` clamped to [8, 32] — so a ≤64-root burst stages in at most two arms.
- **lod F2 — arm drain was FIFO by insert time**: pending entries now sort by `shadowCastAxisDistance(root.position, playerLocalX, playerLocalZ)` ascending — the nearest (most visually impactful) roots' depth variants link first.
- **lod F3+F4 (H) — in-band cold-link on mesh add under a casting root**: a mesh mounted under an already-band-1 root (packaged commit, appearance rebuild, lazy mount) linked its never-staged depth variant inside a presented frame — the band-1 fast path skipped the collect entirely. The per-frame sync now consults `shadowCasterPolicyDirty(root)`: a dirty in-band policy pays the `collectUnstagedShadowCasters` walk, and the *meshes* it returns get their cast flag withheld individually (the root keeps its band; only cold casters wait). Post-withhold the root is re-dirtied via `invalidateShadowCasterPolicy` — otherwise the arm's identical-opts restore early-outs at the `!state.dirty` fast path and the withheld meshes stay shadowless forever. The stage queues iff `unstaged.length > 0` — a `changed`-only noise entry no longer pays the ceremony.
- **popin F1 — arm's unstaged collect re-censused the whole scene per arm**: `collectUnstagedShadowCasters` inside the arm computed `lightCensusSignature` over the live scene per call, and `compileShadowDepthPipelines` re-derived it inside the pass. The arm's memoized `_shadowCensusForFrame` value now threads into both (new `options.lightSigOverride` consumed at mark time — safe: every scene light mounts permanently at boot; authored GLB lights are rejected upstream).
- **popin F2 (M) — release resumed every parked commit in one microtask flush**: `releaseOpeningGraphPublication` resolved the single gate promise; every parked `waitForOpeningGraphPublicationRelease` continuation then ran its commit tail (traverses, `packagedShadowSync`, pipeline links) back-to-back in one task — the W64 port doubled the parked population. Continuations now resume through a render-scoped FIFO driven by `armCallbackAfterPresent` — ≤4 per presented frame, arrival order preserved, `assertGateOwnerCurrent` re-checked at resume so stale-generation parks still abort.
- **popin F3 — `rebuildShipMesh` paid `disposeObject` inside the emit**: the `ship:appearanceChanged` handler traversed+freed the old hull synchronously mid-emit (the same mass-despawn class). The corpse now rides `_despawnDisposeQueue`'s bounded per-frame drain like `entity:destroyed` corpses — detached synchronously, GL tail spread.
- **wart — per-tick rapier warn during init backoff**: `_syncOptionalBackend` minted a fresh `createRapierCollisionWorld` every tick while `loadRapierCompatRuntime` sat in its failure-backoff window → `console.warn` ~60/s for up to 120 s. New `rapierRuntimeBlocked()` predicate; the sync skips minting while blocked (self-heals when the window expires; the warn now fires only on real attempts).
- **boot F1 (M) — the SG-02 lane's symmetric gap**: `_updateSg02DynamicAuthority` is the *default* backend (`rapier-dynamic`) and minted a doomed `createSg02DynamicBodyOwner` every physics tick through the same backoff window — per-tick init-chain allocation + `'SG-02 dynamic authority failed'` warn churn inside the boot gate's 20 s bound and mid-flight. Same `rapierRuntimeBlocked()` gate ahead of the mint; the existing `!this._sg02` tail owns the blocked-frame diags.
- **boot F3 (L) — failure streak never decayed + backoff hid the root cause**: `rapierRuntimeFailures` reset only on success, so transient faults hours apart in a long-lived process accumulated into "consecutive" backoffs; and attempts ≥3 logged only the backoff text, masking the real import/init error. The streak now decays after `RAPIER_INIT_BACKOFF_CAP_MS` (120 s) and the backoff rejection appends `; last error: <message>`.
- **boot F2 — evaluated, skipped (latent, L)**: `loadRapierCompatRuntime({importModule, globalObject})` injection shares the global promise/counters — a test-harness fake could poison production lanes in-process. No in-tree caller passes custom args (rapier-csp tests exercise the CSP helpers, not the importer); a keyed cache is documented as the fix if a real consumer appears.

Verify: golden `47a` **bit-identical** (`892f88c9…`); focused suites green — shadow-depth-admission 27/27 + shadow-caster-policy, opening-mesh-defer, authored-upgrade-queue-lifecycle, living-hull-presentation, renderer-owner-lifetime, live-sector-cook-owner-runtime (76/76 combined), rapier-csp-runtime, physics-authority-cache, sg02-physics-liveness 32/32, sg02-init-lifecycle + contact-sanity-recovery 11/11. Residual reds stash-verified identical on pre-batch HEAD `c58e6c7ef`: `floating-origin-rapier` #2, `post-opening-pipeline-admission` ×2 (preexisting branch reds).

- **hitches F2 (M) — correlated chunk-slack expiry on large displacement**: a gate-seam/dock/teleport jump exhausted every submitted chunk's verdict slack in the same `applyInstanceChunkPolicies` pass → O(total submitted instances) recompute inside an already-heavy transition frame. `nearestSubmittedInstanceMetricsMemoized` now honors `options.recomputeBudget` — a `{remaining: 32}` counter shared per pass: keys-match displacement recomputes past the cap return the stale `nearest` for one frame (the ±10WU verdict slack absorbs it) and retry next pass; mismatched/first memos are correctness recomputes and always run.
- **hitches F3 (L) — per-root repeated keyed lookups in frame sync**: ~6 `geometry.getAttribute(...)` keyed lookups per shielded ship per frame now memoize per mesh via a `WeakMap` (`shieldAuxAttrs` — attribute refs are fixed at mesh creation, so it survives capacity growth and the detached-replacement activate path); the four per-root `entities.get(playerId)` re-fetches in `syncEntityViews` now consume the pass-hoisted `framePlayer`; `camera.updateProjectionMatrix()` is gated on the camera's frustum-param key (`fov|aspect|left|right|top|bottom|near|far|zoom`) instead of running unconditionally per frame — `captureCullCameraState` still compares matrixWorld+projection elements so a stale matrix would surface as cameraDirty anyway.
- **hitches F1 — evaluated, deferred (L effort, L impact, M risk)**: the snapshot fence repacks every active presentation slot every frame (~13 typed-array stores + index insert per slot, tens of µs–0.1 ms on dense sectors). The lane's fix — a persistent slot→snapshot index with membership epochs — is the only sound version: the cheap gates are each unsound (the world's dirty journal is consumed by `syncEntityViews`' `clearDirty` before pack time; `revisions[slot]` is a source revision, not a write counter; a memcpy fast path needs a provable identical-membership+unchanged epoch the world doesn't currently mint). Documented for a future focused lane rather than rushed in-line.

Verify: golden `47a` **bit-identical** (`892f88c9…`); focused suites green — instance-chunk-submit-policy, ship-aux single-pass + prepare-frame-capacity, asteroid-instance camera-quantize + structure (9/9). Residual red stash-verified identical on the pre-batch HEAD: `ship-aux-dirty-ranges` #1 (`2520 !== 472` — preexisting range pin drift).

### W66 — staging session reuse, queued-root collect skip, unified per-mesh withhold, packaged-prop deadline glass, stale-gate re-park, dirtySeq recollect gate, OFF→ON withhold, bounded rapier defer, paced resume drain, despawn cap scaling (all four lanes)

All four lanes returned `saturated: false` (popin lane's own F3 predated this wave's queued-root collect skip — verified identical mechanism, already landed).

- **lod F1 (H) — every arm re-paid the fixed staging ceremony**: `compileShadowDepthPipelines`' whole-scene light traverse, ~15-20 home captures, staging mint, census warm render, and full restore ran per 8-32-root arm — a promotion burst's near-fixed cost multiplied by its arm count. New `createShadowDepthStagingSession`: consecutive arms share ONE staging scene holding cloned light proxies (the live scene's lights are never reparented across presents — `light.clone()` per census light plus fresh target objects, so live lighting never blinks; program keys bake type counts + fog, not identity, so clone proxies key identically). Keyed on the live light-census signature — a sig drift or shadow disable tears it down; the warm census render happens once at mint; `slice(unstaged)` per arm is now just add→matrix→render→mark→evict. Falls back to the single-shot compile when the session can't mint (warm-up throw → warn + legacy path). Kills on drain-empty, sig drift, and `_syncShadowMapEnabled` off.
- **lod F2 (M) — queued withheld roots re-paid `collectUnstagedShadowCasters` every presented frame**: the per-frame checked sync called from `syncEntityViews` re-walked every still-pending root's subtree each frame until its arm slice landed (~log2(n) frames for bursts, exactly during promotion storms). Queued band≠1 roots keep their whole-tree withhold but skip the signature collect; queued band-1 roots distinguish our own post-withhold `invalidateShadowCasterPolicy` re-dirty (`STAGE_SELF_DIRTY_KEY`) from genuine mesh swaps — genuine dirt still collects. A `_withheldDepthCasters` Map<root,Set> reapplies the exact withheld set each frame so re-sync can't un-withhold; the arm's detach/restore loops clear both marks.
- **lod F3 (M) — promotion withheld the whole root**: queued promotions previously demoted every caster under the root until staging. Promotions now share the per-mesh withhold — `syncShadowCasterPolicy` stamps band/opts naturally (promotion → band-1) and only the genuinely unstaged meshes flip `castShadow=false`; staged siblings keep casting through the stage window.
- **lod F4 (partial) — settings toggle paid one synchronous whole-scene collect+compile**: `_stageShadowDepthOnSettingEnable`'s OFF→ON flip ran an unbounded traverse+ceremony inside the settings handler. Now groups unstaged casters by `shadowPolicyRootOf` (nearest ancestor owning a policy record, else direct scene child) and queues them through the same arm queue — bounded slices across presents, identical restore semantics.
- **popin F1 (M) — packaged-prop commit never got the deadline-glass early-out**: `visualOverrides` called `waitForOpeningGraphPublicationRelease()` bare — no `entity` → no `entityOnDeadlineGlass` early-out and no post-first-picture recheck race → an on-glass packaged prop (TOW cargo pod, scenario prop) parked the full ~1.5s publication hold while `hideProceduralPropDrawables` left the husk on glass. Now passes `{...mintedAdmissionOptions, entity: liveEntity || entity}` — the deadline glass early-out + the asyncAdmission/aborted-admission guards the sibling commits already carry.
- **popin F2 (M) — stale-gate drain leak**: resume-queue entries parked under a released gate still drained during the NEXT freeze's hold window — sector jumps never bump `admissionRunGeneration`, so nothing distinguished "released under G0" from "should re-park behind G2". Queue entries now stamp the gate promise they parked on; at drain, if `openingGraphPublicationFrozen` and the live gate differs, the entry re-chains behind the live gate instead of resolving into the hold.
- **popin F4 (L) — unbounded postTask on the resume drain + recheck loop**: `driveOpeningPublicationResume` and the entity recheck armed bare `armCallbackAfterPresent` — on `scheduler.postTask`-but-no-yield engines a saturated main thread could starve both loops indefinitely. Both now pass `{idleBoundMs: 48}` (the pipelineReadiness convention) — the drain/recheck still runs post-present, just never starves.
- **boot B-F1 (M) — explicit prepares instant-denied inside a residual rapier backoff**: `prepareBackend` consulted `rapierRuntimeBlocked()` only to *skip* the init mint — a post-backoff prepare still returned false for the whole ≤120s window, and a New Game/Continue retry inside it silently bounced the deterministic backend. Now: when the residual is ≤ `initTimeoutMs`, the prepare waits it out (token-guarded, matching the reset-bounce contract) then mints a real attempt. New export `rapierRuntimeBlockedRemainingMs()`.
- **boot B-F5 (L) — rapier decay check `>` instead of `>=`**: an init attempt landing exactly at the 120s window edge decayed nothing; now `>=`.
- **boot B-F3 (M) — OFF→ON queued roots missed the withhold**: the settings-enable path queued group roots for staged compiles but left them drawing unlinked depth variants — the same cold-link class the promotion path covers. Now withholds `castShadow=false` at queue time (unioned into `_withheldDepthCasters`, dirtySeq-stamped), restore on drain.
- **boot B-F4 (L) — `_armDepthStage` postTask unbounded**: same starvation class as popin F4; now `{idleBoundMs: 48}`.
- **boot B-F2 + hitches F1 (H) — selfDirty stamp can't distinguish genuine re-dirt**: the boolean latch treated its own post-withhold invalidate as unstageable dirt, so a queued band-1 root that legitimately re-dirtied (packaged swap, authored commit) either cold-linked the new meshes or, once that hole closed, paid a whole-subtree `syncShadowCasterPolicy` every presented frame. `invalidateShadowCasterPolicy` now bumps a `dirtySeq` generation: the queue stamps the generation it withhold at, and a genuine invalidate bumps past the stamp → recollect + union into the withheld cache + re-stamp; matching stamps → the cached-reuse branch re-stamps flags and returns early (dirty persists for the arm's restore — the sync's only cleaner). Detach/restore paths assign `false`, which the typeof-number check treats as -1 (no stamp) — backward compatible.
- **hitches F2 (M) — publication-resume drain measured nothing**: `OPENING_PUBLICATION_RESUME_BATCH` resolved up to N=8 waiters per arm unconditionally — each resumed tail's sync prefix stacks into the same post-present task. The drive is now async: after each `resolve()` it `await`s a microtask so the tail's microtask prefix runs first, measures the wall cost, and stops the batch once `PACED_FRAME_BUDGET_MS` (4ms) is spent — dual-signaled on the shared paced ledger `pacedFrameSpend()` for tails that debit it themselves. `PACED_FRAME_BUDGET_MS` exported as `GLTF_COMPILE_FRAME_MS`'s alias so all per-frame paced budgets name one contract.
- **hitches F3 (L) — despawn dispose count cap didn't scale with backlog**: `drainDespawnDisposeQueue` ran the fixed 8-per-drain against a queue that mass-despawn can push hundreds into (the up-front `Math.min(queue.length, 64)` arc-share). Now `max(8, ceil(remaining/4))` — bounded ~4 drains like `drainPresentationTail`.
- **hitches F4 (L) — `rebuildShipMesh` skipped the diagnostics publish the destroyed path queues**: dead-entry evicts inside the sweep are invisible to the share-driving poll unless something queues `_queueAssetResidencyDiagnosticsPublish` — now queued after `noteShadowMeshRemoved`, matching `destroyedShip`'s shape.

### W67 — dirtySeq latch re-arm, mesh-capped stage legs, rendered-set census sig, fresh arm census, resolved OFF→ON opts, frozen-root transform re-dirty (lod + popin lanes; boot/hitches in flight)

Lanes reporting so far returned `saturated: false`.

- **lod F1 (H) — dirtySeq self-latch never re-armed: every post-withhold band-1 root paid the collect + full sync every presented frame, forever**: two holes — (a) the arm's restore loop wrote `STAGE_SELF_DIRTY_KEY = false` → `stampedSeq=-1` → the `dirtySeq > stampedSeq && dirtySeq > 0` gate stayed true permanently; (b) a genuine invalidate whose collect returned `[]` never re-stamped (the stamp only minted inside `unstaged.length>0`) → same permanent per-frame collect+traverse, ~0.5-3ms/frame per root and growing monotonically with every authored swap. The collect's generation now stamps on every evaluated band-1 collect (empty included, queued or not — an unstageable dirty still settles), and the arm's restore stamps the live seq it covered. Detach deliberately keeps `false`: a detached root never staged, so a remount must recollect.
- **lod F2 (M) — stage legs bounded by casters, not roots**: the slice cap counted roots (≤32), so one private render could pay a station-family burst's ~2000 draws atomically inside the post-present task. Legs now cap at `SHADOW_DEPTH_ARM_MESH_CAP=128` meshes; a post-stage recollect maps still-unmarked meshes back to their policy roots and requeues those roots (restoring them would un-withhold casters whose variant never linked). Fully covered roots restore as before.
- **lod F3 (M) — `lightCensusSignature` keyed only a bare light count**: `l{n}|f{f}` was blind to same-count type swaps (dir→point changes real program keys while the sig stood still → stale marks trusted → cold links in presented frames) and counted invisible/masked lights that bake no counts (spurious session kill+re-mint). Now keys the rendered-membership set — per-type counts + castShadow + layers mask per light (invisible-ancestor lights excluded, sorted for traverse-order independence) + fog kind — over-sensitive only to mask churn on rendered lights, which costs a re-mint, never a cold link.
- **lod F4 (L) — arm minted the staging session under the `_viewSyncSeq` census memo**: the memo's contract is intra-pass dedup for withhold evals; the arm runs between passes, so a light add/remove between the last sync and the arm minted under a stale sig. Now `lightCensusSignature(scene)` fresh at arm time.
- **lod F5 (L) — OFF→ON queue used generic opts**: `pending.set(root, {lodLevel:null, entity:null})` made the arm's restore resolve `allowCast=true` unconditionally (one frame of band-1 over-cast on every out-of-ortho root) and stamp `lodLevel=null` (a forced traverse next sync). Queue entries now resolve `root.userData.entityId → entities.get` and `root.userData.lod.level` at queue time — the same sources the per-view sync uses.

- **popin F1 (H/S) — staging restore never re-dirtied mount-frozen meshes**: `session.slice`/`compileShadowDepthPipelines` reparent casters into the staging scene, run `staging.updateMatrixWorld(true)` — recomputing `matrixWorld` under an identity parent AND consuming `matrixWorldNeedsUpdate` — then `restoreObjectHome` re-seats with no re-dirty. Vendored `updateMatrixWorld` skips clean `sfMatrixFrozen` subtrees, so a mount-frozen mesh rendered at its local transform as world position permanently (stray fragment at sector origin / absent piece on stations/places/packaged — exactly the W63-66 promotion cohort). `restoreObjectHome` now stamps `matrixWorldNeedsUpdate` — covers all ~15 capture/restore sites; `add()` already unfroze ancestor marks so the next walk recomputes under the real parent.
- **popin F2 (M/S) — `_applyFrameOriginRebase` wrote `position.x/z += delta` on every `_meshes` root with no `updateMatrix()`**: every other pose writer runs the `matrixAutoUpdate===false → updateMatrix()` hook; mount-frozen roots never track the origin shift and render displaced by exactly (dx,dz) until their next seat write (never — field rocks are covered by the `frameOriginSeq` poseRow walk, non-field frozen roots weren't). Same hook added.
- popin lane's W66-machinery checklist: all 7 items PASS (session clones mint identical keys + private map; sig-drift kill→re-mint; every kill path restores cast flags; dirtySeq monotonic; promotion restores re-read live opts; ≥1 progress/arm; bounded rapier defer + ceil despawn cap).

W67 hitches + boot closed the wave — all four lanes `saturated: false` (streak 0/3).

- **hitches F1 (H) — the depth arm's non-render work ran unbounded inside one post-present task**: whole-scene census + ≤32-root collect + possible session re-mint + leg slice + a second whole-scene census on the capped leftover + ≤32 restores — ~5-20ms JS landing adjacent to the next presented frame. The collect now chunks per-root under a 4ms deadline (un-reached roots requeue still-withheld), the restore loop requeues under the same rule (staged leftovers stay withheld one more arm — never a cold link), the arm debits the shared paced ledger, and the capped-leftover recollect reuses the arm's own census sig (the second whole-scene traverse is gone).
- **hitches F2 (H) — private per-slice clocks never shared the paced ledger**: compile drains, the compose driver and the resume drain were the only slicers on `pacedFrameSpend()`; residency reconcile/poll (4ms+4ms), the deferred-enter splice (4ms), hold-exempt beats (4ms+2ms floor), emit/presentation drains, despawn dispose (2ms), admission slices (3ms) and the depth arm stacked ~25-30ms of paced JS inside one presented frame on busy arrivals/jumps. Every continue-check now consults `pacedFrameSpend()` and every drain debits `notePacedFrameSpend(real spend)` — min-1-step progress semantics preserved at every site (the ledger check always sits post-step / after minItems so nothing starves; `usePacedLedger` is opt-in on `shouldContinueAdmissionSlice` and Infinity/loading budgets bypass as before).
- **hitches F3 (M) — undrawable-forever leftovers spun arm legs**: a root whose whole unmarked remainder was offered to a leg and came back unmarked (geometry-less stub, unreadable layer, material-invisible — the classes `collectUnstagedShadowCasters` collects but the stage can never draw) re-queued every arm: collect → slice → draw-nothing → recollect. Such roots now park (`_parkedDepthStageRoots`): flags stay withheld (the cold-link contract holds), parked counts as queued in the checked sync so the cached-reuse branch still re-stamps flags, a genuine dirtySeq bump unparks via the normal collect path, a fresh empty collect releases the park so live flags restore, and detached parks sweep at arm time.
- **hitches F4 (M) — shadow OFF→ON paid the whole-scene signature collect inside the click**: `collectUnstagedShadowCasters` mints per-material signature strings + probes the staged set for every caster scene-wide — ~5-15ms in the settings task on a dense sector, while the arm re-collects every slice anyway. The click now runs the flag-only `collectPotentialShadowCastSubjects` enumeration (exported for it) — the per-slice recollect re-derives the genuinely-unstaged set inside bounded arm work.
- **hitches F5 (S-M) — evaluated-and-deferred**: fusing the genuine band-1 re-dirty's two subtree traverses (signature collect + policy flag stamp) would need the collect to apply castShadow flags mid-walk — the withheld/qualifier predicates resolve there, but `syncShadowCasterPolicy` also stamps band state + non-mesh opts the fusion would have to preserve exactly. Recorded for a later wave; the check ordering (queue-withheld first, stamp after) makes the splice subtle for ~2 subtree traverses per real re-dirty.
- **hitches F6 (L) — despawn drain stacked on spent frames + comment math lied**: `drainDespawnDisposeQueue` ignored the paced ledger entirely and its comment claimed ~4 drains to clear a 600-item wipe (the geometric tail actually needs ~14). Now consults the ledger before starting (a spent window keeps corpses one more frame), debits its real spend, and the comment states the true bound.
- **hitches F7 (L-M) — signature string churn per collect**: `casterDepthSignatures` re-minted the 9-field variant discriminant string per caster-material per collect (~100KB+ per burst frame). The variant substring is interned per material via WeakMap on its raw input bits — mutations re-mint under the new bits, repeat collects reuse.
- **boot F1 (M) — `reset:true` prepares instant-denied inside rapier backoff**: `prepareBackend`'s backoff defer excluded `reset:true` prepares — a New Game click inside an init-failure window (≥2 consecutive failures, 30s+ residual) bounced `PHYSICS_BACKEND_UNAVAILABLE` to menu instantly, while non-reset prepares waited the residual out and minted. In every defer-eligible state (`!_sg02Init && !_sg02`) the reset's `_disableSg02DynamicAuthority` is a pure no-op (no live authority, no token bump), so the exclusion protected nothing — dropped `&& !reset`. The callers' 20s race still bounds conversion; an abandoned wait mints the backend for the next click. (Note: the W66 boot wave's `rapierRuntimeBlockedRemainingMs` defer already covered the continue path; this extends it to the reset caller.)
- Boot lane machinery checklist at this HEAD: staging-session census kills+re-mints, kill paths restore cast flags + buffer hook, dirtySeq monotonic-with-stamp, per-mesh withhold vs mid-arm promote, arm min-1 progress — all PASS.

### W68 — flag-tuple depth collect, parked-root unpark+recheck, present-keyed paced ledger, measured resume drain (lod + popin + boot lanes; hitches in flight)

Lanes reporting so far returned `saturated: false`.

- **lod F1 (H) — the in-frame policy collect re-minted signature strings per caster per frame**: every band-1 re-dirty (and every first withhold) paid `collectUnstagedShadowCasters` — whole-subtree traverse + per-material 9-field signature mints + staged-Set probes — inside the presented frame. Marks now write a per-mesh discriminant tuple (`sfDepthMark`: light sig + geometry ref + morph census + custom depth material + layer mask + kind + material uuid/variant pairs) at `markCastersDepthStaged`, and the frame path runs `collectUnstagedShadowCastersFlag` — the identical unstaged set (signature-capable ∧ not-currently-marked) with zero signature mints. Material mutation/swap, geometry swap, layer flip, and census drift all mismatch → recollect, exactly like the signature path.
- **lod F2 (M) — leg-capped leftover paid a second whole-slice signature collect**: the cap's still-unstaged root lookup now re-checks the collect's own per-root list against `casterDepthMarkCurrent` — the same post-leg verdict (covers mid-slice drift through the CURRENT discriminant) without the second traverse.
- **lod F3 (M) — vendored `eachShadowCaster` scanned the whole caster registry per staging render**: every leg render walked O(#live casters × ancestor hops) of membership skips for a subset of ~128 meshes. Staging scenes now carry `userData.sfShadowCastSubset` (the leg Set); the vendored pass iterates the subset when present — the drawn set is identical (subset members are the reparented casters), non-members skip without the ancestor walk.
- **lod F4 (H) — the paced ledger's 8ms window under-reports on heavy frames**: `PACE_FRAME_WINDOW_MS` anchored on first debit, so a >8ms presented frame minted a fresh wallet mid-frame — effective budget scaled with frame length exactly where it hurts. The wallet is now keyed on a lazily-armed rAF epoch pump (real browser frames only — headless/test hosts keep the wall-clock window, so no stubbed-rAF hang or frozen-epoch livelock).
- **lod F5 (S) — arm preamble was unmeasured**: `armStartedAt` now stamps at the callback top — the pending sweep, parked sweep, nearest-first sort, and cap splice all debit the paced ledger.
- **lod F6 (M) — hold-exempt commit pumps could do zero work on a spent beat**: the `commitBounded && (clock || ledger)` gates sat pre-step, so a beat arriving already spent broke with zero enqueue/kick steps — exempt builds could queue forever inside a hold. Both pumps now get ≥1 step per beat (post-step check), matching the collect pump's contract.
- **lod F7 (M) — parked-detach sweep left stale bookkeeping**: the `_parkedDepthStageRoots` sweep now clears `STAGE_SELF_DIRTY_KEY` + `_withheldDepthCasters` like the pending-detach sweep — a remount used to inherit a "still withheld" verdict and skip the re-collect. Parked `seq` is also live now (see boot F1).
- **boot F1 = popin F1 (H, two lanes converged) — parked roots could never unpark**: parked roots stamp `castBand=0` at their next sync, so the `band!==1 → !queued` collect gate stayed shut forever — the `seq` field the park stored for exactly this compare was dead code, and a re-dirtied parked root wedged withheld permanently (e.g. a caster drifting inside the shadow ortho after a fat mount burst parked it). The checked sync now deletes the parked entry when `dirtySeq > parkedEntry.seq` *before* the queued gating, so the normal collect/withhold path runs.
- **popin F2 (M) — parked band-1 roots withheld until an unrelated re-dirty**: drawability flips that never invalidate the policy (visible toggle, LOD swap, ortho drift) left withheld casters dark indefinitely. Parked entries now re-collect on a bounded cadence (every ~96 sync visits, flag-collect) — non-empty unstaged re-queues the root through the normal withhold path; an empty collect releases the park so live flags restore.
- **popin F3 (M) — resume-drain measured only the resolution hop**: `driveOpeningPublicationResume`'s `await Promise.resolve()` measured ~µs while the caller's commit tail chained through microtasks unmeasured — N commit tails stacked per arm task with the brake blind to the cost it spaced. The drain now yields a macrotask and measures elapsed (capturing the commit chain), debiting `spentMs` AND the shared ledger.
- **popin/boot F4 (M) — paced-ledger debit gaps closed**: completing reconcile/poll slices now debit their spend unconditionally (was suspension-only — a final leg ran free), and the resume drain debits its measured hop (see popin F3).
- **boot F2 (M) — overlapping SG-02 prepares**: (a) a deferred prepare that wakes to a token mismatch used to return `false` — the live caller's `waitForPhysics` read it as backend failure → deterministic bounce under overlapping Continue/reset clicks; it now adopts and awaits the winner's `_sg02Init` on the same envelope and reports its readiness. (b) a `reset:true` prepare could stamp a fresh token over a pending init and mint a second concurrent wasm world-init; it now awaits the pending init's settle (bounded by `initTimeoutMs`) before disabling.
- **boot F3 (M) — commit sites ran under a newer freeze**: `waitForOpeningGraphPublicationRelease` released a waiter when its captured gate popped, but a newer freeze could own the window by the time the commit continuation landed (the resume tail chains through paced queues). The wait now re-parks under the live gate after each resume — release only when no gate holds — covering all commit sites at once.
- **popin F6 (L-M) — `authoredReadableFallbackLayer` cast asymmetry**: the fallback layer is excluded from every depth-staging collector but `syncShadowCasterPolicy` still cast it — a fallback mesh flipped ON post-mount minted its depth variant inside a presented frame (the cold link the arm exists to prevent, on the hero Kestrel body). The policy now withholds cast on the flag (receive stays).
- **popin F7 (L) — mesh-build skip prefix was unbounded**: `itemsDone: built` never counted skips, so `minItems` stayed unfired and a pure-skip prefix scanned the whole queue inside one call regardless of slice/ledger. The drain now passes `itemsDone: examined` (dequeued rows) — same ≥1-row guarantee, bounded scan.

### W69 — despawn-drain aging cap, decorrelated parked rechecks, node-budgeted collects, mid-arm detach cleanup (lod lane; popin/boot/hitches in flight)

- **lod F1 (M) — `drainDespawnDisposeQueue` was the only paced lane with no starvation bound**: `pacedFrameSpend() >= budget → return 0` on every call meant sustained heavy frames deferred corpse/GL reclaim indefinitely while the depth arm and glTF drain both cap skips at 2. `DESPAWN_DISPOSE_LEDGER_MAX_SKIPS = 2` — after two skipped drains the lane runs its bounded min-item slice anyway.
- **lod F2 (M) — parked recheck cadence was correlated**: every root parked in one arm got `recheck: 96` and decremented per entity-view sync, so same-arm cohorts expired in the same pass → up to 8 flag-collect subtree walks + over-cover withholds + arm refill in one presented frame, every ~96 syncs. `recheck` now mints/re-arms at `96 + (parkedMap.size % 32)` — cohort expiries spread across ~32 syncs.
- **lod F3 (M) — the collect cap counted roots, not nodes**: one fat packaged subtree paid an unbounded traverse inside a single sync call regardless of the 8-root cap. All flag collects in a pass now share `SHADOW_DEPTH_PASS_NODE_CAP = 4096` visited nodes; a subtree that outruns the budget aborts mid-walk (`_walkBudgetAbort` → `UNSTAGED_COLLECT_OVER_COVER`) and over-covers like the root-cap path — the arm re-derives for real.
- **lod F4 (L) — detached-mid-arm roots leaked bookkeeping**: the restore loop's `!root.parent` continue and the requeue tail's non-parent drop skipped `_withheldDepthCasters`/`STAGE_SELF_DIRTY_KEY` cleanup — the renderer's plain Maps retained the detached root + withheld mesh Set until GC (self-healing on remount, retention-only). Both drop paths now clear the same bookkeeping the arm-start sweeps do.

W69 hitches lane closed its share — `saturated: false` (streak 0/3). F5 (per-root subtree cap) was already covered by this wave's lod F3 node budget; F4 (janky-frame min-1 aggregate) is documented-and-deferred: a global cap across the min-1 lanes needs per-lane starvation counters — a bigger design move (~15-40ms worst-case all-queues-hot aggregate; the epoch wallet itself can't over-host).

- **hitches F1 (H) — cast-only policy traversals paid a whole-scene receiver recount**: `syncShadowCasterPolicy` returns `true` for every withhold/restore/band flip, and the per-frame caller marked the receiver tally dirty → next presented frame walked the entire scene recounting `receiveShadow`, though withholds provably rewrite castShadow only. The traverse now sums its own receiveShadow flips into `options.out.receiverDelta`; `noteShadowPolicyChanged` accepts the delta-carrying result — `noteDelta(d)` settles the count exactly (zero delta → zero marks), boolean callers keep the dirty-recount contract, and `_markShadowReceiversDirty` splits into `_shadowMapDirty` (still needed — the map re-renders on caster changes) vs the tally path.
- **hitches F2 (M) — resume drain double-accounted the paced ledger**: `driveOpeningPublicationResume`'s `setTimeout(0)` window deliberately contains the resumed commit tail, but that tail already debits itself through `notePacedFrameSpend` inside the same window — `elapsed` then charged it a second time, halving the intended 4ms drain (~1 resume/arm, ~4× more hops). The drain now brakes on ledger delta alone (`pacedFrameSpend() - entrySpend >= budget`) and charges only the hop's non-debiting residue.
- **hitches F3 (M) — parked-cohort re-arm re-synchronized**: the W69 `96 + parkedMap.size % 32` jitter staggers mint-order correctly, but the expiry re-arm evaluated `parkedMap.size` once per pass — every co-expiring root got the identical value and re-synchronized (the exact cycle the lane flagged). Both stamp sites now mint from a per-stamp counter — legal because the recheck cadence is a best-effort drawability backstop, not a correctness deadline.

Merge: master `1a990b445` in cleanly — upstream D130 split the shared ledger walk into per-side far/rock discs; the `tolerateMiss`/'covered' ride semantics ported onto each walk independently (per-key `collectRadius` stamps the queried disc; covered = version-match + geometric containment of the needed disc; loose remints serve an empty scratch only when the corresponding disc is dead). 6 new CI reds A/B-verified upstream canon on `1a990b445` (sg06-production-ports, ui-budgets baseline, ship-purchase-guidance, input-modalities, sg08-render-vfx, arcade-structural-fx).

Verify: golden `47a` **bit-identical** (`892f88c9…` — also identical on clean master `1a990b445`); focused suites green — shadow-receiver-tally 8/8 (new delta regression test), shadow-caster-policy 9/9, shadow-caster-registry, shadow-depth-admission 19/19, renderer-shadow-frame 5/5, event-bus-contract 31/31, admission-slice-budget 12/12, hold-exempt-scan + first-flight-hold-exempt, contact-shadow-dirty-ranges 3/3, bounded-warm-root-park 3/3, shadow-present-cadence 6/6, inf-073-resume 2/2, inf-087 4/4, perf-combat-count-gates 12/12, live-sector-cook-owner-runtime 50/50.

W69 popin + boot lanes closed the wave — both `saturated: false` (streak 0/3 across all 4 lanes).

- **popin F1 (M-H) — deadline-glass hoist unreachable under a saturated ledger**: `_drainMeshBuildQueue` examines ~1 row/call when the paced ledger is spent (min-1), so `built` never reaches `buildBudget` and the count-cap hoist never runs — glass work behind a skip prefix waited ~prefix-length calls. The `shouldContinueAdmissionSlice`-false break now hoists once per drain (ordering only; the ledger still bounds builds through the same check on later calls).
- **popin F2 (M) — `postFirstPicture` latched at wait-entry**: a commit parked before the first picture (all opening-cook commits) kept `postFirstPicture === false` for the whole freeze chain → no glass bypass, no recheck failsafe — an entity crossing the presented glass mid-chain showed stand-ins for the entire chain. The failsafe now wraps every entity-scoped wait and reads `firstPlayableFrameAt` lazily inside the recheck.
- **popin F3 (M-L) — re-parked waiters paid a whole gate per freeze hop**: waiters now carry `firstArmedAt` through every `awaitGate` re-park; past 2s parked age the requeue head-inserts (drain-side and caller-side) so an aged waiter wins the first inter-freeze window instead of trailing arrival order again.
- **popin F4 (M) — fixed resume batch didn't scale with backlog**: `OPENING_PUBLICATION_RESUME_BATCH` stays the floor; the arm now runs `min(16, max(4, ceil(queue/8)))` per batch — a long-throttled tab's accrued resumes pay out in one window instead of a multi-second ≤4/present pop-in tail. The ledger brake still bounds spend.
- **boot F1 (H) — bare-rAF arms gated every model load and ship build under a hidden tab**: `scheduleGltfParse`/`scheduleGltfCompile` (initial arm + 3 re-arm sites) and `composeYield` were rAF-only → a hidden/occluded tab stalled the drain wholesale until the 20s/180s envelopes bounced. `armFrameDrain` dual-arms rAF+setTimeout(48) (rAF wins under real frames → identical scheduling; starved hosts converge at timer cadence — the same pattern frame-wait/restore/armCallbackAfterPresent/yieldLiveSectorGpu already carry).
- **boot F2 (M) — `prepareBackend` internal waits inherited the 60s default under 20s caller races**: `_sg02InitArmedAt` stamps at mint; the adopt and reset-settle joins now bound by `initTimeoutMs - (now - armedAt)` (a pending init past its own envelope is dead, not re-payed a full window — closes the stale-capture wedge tax); the post-mint settle keeps the full `SG02_INIT_PREPARE_TIMEOUT_MS` bring-up; both user gates plumb `initTimeoutMs: 20000` so a backoff residual in (20s,60s] fast-fails instead of dwell-then-bounce.
- **boot F3 (M) — frozen rAF epoch read permanently over-budget**: `pacePumpFiredAt` stamps inside the epoch pump; `paceEpochNow()` returns -1 when the pump is >64ms stale (~4 missed frames) → the 8ms wall-clock window re-keys the ledger honestly in a hidden tab instead of every ledger-aware lane collapsing to min-1 for the session.

### W70 — retriable-warm coverage release, live-gate re-read, arm restore tally delta, emit-slice suspension (lod/popin/boot lanes; hitches saturated — streak 1/3)

W70 **hitches lane reported `saturated: true`** — the second lane-level saturation of the campaign (W47 lod was first). Streak: 1/3. Its residual findings still landed below (F3a converged with lod F1, F1b with boot F3).

- **popin F1 (H) — warm coverage rows never released on failed kicks**: the launch warm marked each enemyId coverage-claimed at kick time and only unmarked on synchronous build throws — a kick settling a retriable outcome (readmission-marked, cancelled, orphaned, errored, deferred, cooloff) left the row pinned forever, so the first live spawn of that archetype paid the in-round compose + program link the warm existed to pre-pay. `SWARM_WARM_RETRIABLE_OUTCOMES` names the settle vocabulary that never warmed: kicks now carry their `lootTableId` and unmark via the shared `unmarkOnRetriableOutcome` on `.then` (launch lane) and through `pendingAttachmentEnemyIds` + a post-allSettled sweep (deferred lane), both per-warm idempotent via `failedCoverageMarks`/the existing `unmarked` Set so a ship+hulk pair releases its shared row once. `'unavailable'` stays claimed on purpose — a genuinely missing GLB must not re-decode every dwell — and `'invalid-upgrade-request'` means the request was malformed, so a retry resolves identically.
- **popin F2 (M) — the re-park loop read a captured gate method**: `waitForOpeningGraphPublicationRelease` captured `render.waitForOpeningGraphPublicationRelease` at wait-entry — a gate object re-armed between freezes could leave the stale method minting an already-settled promise while a newer freeze held the window → a commit license handed into the middle of the next hold. The live-gate loop now re-fetches the method per iteration (same typeof guard as the first call).
- **boot F1 = lod F3 (M-H) — uncapped legs never ran the leftover audit**: `leftoverByRoot` computed only under `legCapped === true`, so a leg under the 128-mesh cap that offered meshes but never marked them (draw-throw, undrawable-forever) restored their cast flags unproven — the depth variant then linked inside the next presented shadow refresh, the exact cold-link the withhold machinery exists to prevent. The audit now runs on every leg; a fully-offered-but-unmarked mesh takes the park path.
- **lod F1 = hitches F3a (M-H) — the arm's restore traversed without feeding the receiver tally**: `syncShadowCasterPolicy` inside `_armDepthStage`'s restore wrote flags and returned `changed` but the delta never reached `noteShadowPolicyChanged` — subtree churn between withhold and restore silently drifted `_shadowReceiverCount`, and an under-count reaching 0 freezes the shadow refresh until an unrelated dirty recounts. The restore now mints `out`, spreads it over `_shadowPolicyOptions` (retained scratch — spread, not mutate), and feeds `{changed:true, receiverDelta}` like the checked sync.
- **hitches hardening (M) — `_armDepthStage` could wedge `_depthStageScheduled === true`**: a throw between the pending splice and the re-arm left live pending entries behind a permanently-set scheduled flag → the drain wedged and withheld casters stayed dark forever. The re-arm/teardown tail now lives in `finally`.
- **hitches F3b (M) — `swapTo`'s retained-root LOD swap dodged every invalidate seam**: `boundary.remove(prev)`/`boundary.add(next)` in partsLibrary's whole-ship `swapTo` was the one live subtree attach that never bumped `dirtySeq` — a band-1 root queued for staging would trust its stale withheld set while the subtree changed. `swapTo` now invalidates when the tree actually changes; the cached-reuse fast path documents the invariant (every attach/detach must hit an invalidate seam).
- **hitches F1a (M) — the resume drain armed unconditionally**: `driveOpeningPublicationResume` resolved a whole batch inside its post-present task even when the frame's paced budget was already spent — resumed commit tails tolerate deferral (they already waited on the gate). The arm now defers a frame on absolute spend with a `OPENING_PUBLICATION_RESUME_MAX_SKIPS = 2` aging bound — the same contract the depth-stage arm keeps.
- **boot F3 = hitches F1b (M) — `PACE_EPOCH_STALE_MS` was tighter than honest frames**: 64ms (~4 frames at 60fps) flipped the ledger to the 8ms wall-clock window inside ordinary slow frames, letting per-frame spend leak across frame boundaries exactly when frames ran hot. 250ms sits above the worst honest presented frame; a genuinely starved pump still falls back.
- **boot F2 (M) — sliced emits parked while nothing presented**: `sector:enter`/`save:loaded` sliced listener tails drain through the presentation pump — which doesn't exist while suspended, hidden, or booting, so emit tails waited out the whole non-presenting window. `emitSliceSuspended` makes `emit` deliver inline while suspended and `flushSlicedEmits` drains the parked tail + queue at the transition (a dedicated loop — the paced ledger isn't consulted off-frame). The runner plumbs it at mint + both lifecycle transitions.
- **lod F2 (M) — swap seams forced whole-scene receiver recounts**: `onAuthoredAssetSwap`, `syncPackagedBodyShadowPolicy`, and prepared-boundary rollback/dispose all marked `_shadowReceiversDirty` → next presented frame walked the entire scene recounting `receiveShadow`. Where both roots are in hand the bookkeeping is exact: `noteRemoved(fallback)` + `noteAdded(authored)` + the checked sync's own `receiverDelta` (swap), `noteAdded(packaged)` + delta (packaged graft), `noteRemoved(boundary)` (rollback/dispose). `markShadowReceiversDirty` stays for genuinely unknowable diffs (bind, mass-clear, settings).
- **lod F4 (L) — documented the tally contract**: `_noteShadowMeshAdded`/`_noteShadowMeshRemoved` now carry the invariant — every scene add/remove of a flag-carrying subtree must route through them or a dirty mark; an uncounted add makes the next `noteRemoved` over-subtract → 0-clamp → frozen shadow refresh.

Verify: golden `47a` **bit-identical** (`892f88c9…`); focused suites green — event-bus-contract, presentation-runner, shadow-caster-policy 9/9, shadow-caster-registry, shadow-receiver-tally, shadow-depth-admission, renderer-shadow-frame, shadow-present-cadence, crucible-launch-warm-twin-promotion 6/6, survival-arena-roster-prewarm 7/7, bounded-warm-root-park 3/3, d157-packaged-opening-boundary 6/6, d21-crucible-warm-package-residency 3/3, package-cpu-detach 9/9, m6-packaging-parity 6/6, live-ship-visual-package-coverage 6/6, decode-runway-pick 7/7, decode-runway-residency 19/19, encounter-pending-decode-warm 4/4, compile-present-slice 12/12, present-phase-timers, startup-yield-present, flight-present-scheduling 3/3, pq-133-04-r4-presentation 8/8, asteroid-pool-shadow-scope, contact-shadow-dirty-ranges 3/3; residual reds verified identical on clean master `1a990b445` (swarm-deferred-warm roster-count pin 18≠16, sector-prewarm-boundary-generation authored-serial pins ×2 — all reproduce without the batch).

### W71 — warm-coverage status objects + same-dwell re-kick, claimed-outcome inversion, paced cooloff, present-stamp gate (popin/boot/hitches landed; lod auditing — streak 0/3)

All three reported lanes `saturated: false`; the W70 hitches saturation stays a 1-of-3 maximum. Golden baseline re-baselined again: upstream's own sim drift moved the hash to `54edce56…` on clean master `34da071c9` without re-pinning the expected envelope, so the verify semantic is branch-hash == clean-master-hash (both trees exit 1 on the stale `892f88c9` envelope pin).

- **popin F1 (H) — packaged-body kicks could never release coverage**: every failure leg of the packaged `requestAuthoredUpgrade` resolved bare `false`, and `unmarkOnRetriableOutcome` only released `null`/retriable statuses — a transient fetch wedge, decode timeout, or orphaned swap pinned the archetype's coverage row for the rest of the run while the ledger believed it warmed (ship exemplars recover via the release→retry loop; hulk/wreck/drone/gate/pod exemplars never did). The packaged `completion` now resolves status objects like the queued ship path: `{status:'orphaned-before-swap'}` on unmounted legs, `'awaiting-authored-admission'` on owner-inactive readmission marks, `'fallback-after-error'` on load throws/pipeline failures, `'unavailable'` only for the genuinely-absent record/empty package, `'stale-verdict-superseded'` on epoch-superseded legs.
- **hitches F2 ⊃ hitches F1 (H) — the unmark gate failed toward pinning**: the closed `SWARM_WARM_RETRIABLE_OUTCOMES` vocabulary missed `aborted-stalled` (a live instance — stall-aborted jobs settle retriable yet pinned coverage) and any future status minted after the list. Inverted to a keep-claimed terminal set (`completed`, `shell-ready`, `unavailable`, `procedural-settled`, `invalid-upgrade-request`, `authored*`, `same-semantic-fallback*`, primitive `true`) — unknown outcomes now release toward a bounded re-warm instead of a guaranteed in-flight compose.
- **popin F2 (M) — released rows re-warmed only at the NEXT wave's dwell**: a kick settling retriably mid-dwell released its row but nothing re-kicked within the same dwell, so the archetype the warm just declared uncovered still spawned unwarmed this wave. The post-allSettled sweep now collects the released kicks and runs ONE bounded re-kick pass inside the warm's existing deadline before `swarmDeferredWarm.pending` flips — each retry re-arms its own exemplar boundary (readmission status or the bounded `retryFailedAuthoredAdmission` path), re-marks the row at dispatch, and carries its own release allowance so exemplars sharing an enemyId can't double-release; a second retriable settle stops — two strikes is the next dwell's signal. `unmark()` now decrements by `1 + retryMintCount` so the catch-unmark releases retry-minted claims exactly.
- **hitches F3 (M-H) — `buildComposedShipAsync` never read `pacedFrameSpend()`**: the one frame-paced slicer not on the shared 4ms ledger gate — a frame already spent by earlier rAF-batch work still got a full 8/12ms compose slice appended (×2 during opening). The yield predicate now includes `pacedFrameSpend() >= PACED_FRAME_BUDGET_MS`; per-step min-progress preserved (the check sits between steps).
- **boot F1 (M) — `nextPaintSliced`'s freshness check was blind to the live present stream**: `lastPaintAt` stamped only inside `nextPaint`'s own finish, so ~10 gate boundaries across New Game/Continue each paid a full double-rAF (≈0.15–0.4s serialized dead-time per transition) while the runner presented continuously. The runner now invokes `deps.onPresented` on accepted presents only (suspended/restoring windows correctly stop stamping — D35/D36 semantics preserved) and main stamps `lastPaintAt` from it.
- **boot F2 (L) — `save:loaded` drain lacked the re-arm bound `flushSlicedEmits` carries**: a listener cycle re-arming sliced emits would stall the restore generator while still painting (latent — no live cycle exists today). Added the same `guard>64` iteration cap.
- **popin F3 (L) — `regrade-evict-cooloff` re-armed the on-glass trigger instantly**: a sustained marginal graze reposted guaranteed-refused enqueues every rendered frame — the repost never lands in the domain the repost cap counts, so the paced set is the only break. Added to `REFUSAL_TRIGGER_PACED_STATUSES` (1s re-arm, visibility-aware).

Verify: golden `47a` **bit-identical** (`54edce56…` — clean master `34da071c9` produces the identical hash); focused suites green — swarm-deferred-warm 8/8, authored-admission-recovery 9/9, authored-pipeline-yield, event-bus-contract 31/31, crucible-swarm 34/34, d157-packaged-opening-boundary, d21-crucible-warm-package-residency 3/3; modulepreload in sync; probe-sector-arrival-admission PASS locally (CI red = starved-runner flake — the same station publishes at +5s here).

W71 **lod lane closed the wave — `saturated: false`** (all 4 lanes unsaturated, streak stays 0/3). The W70 "exact receiver bookkeeping" contract was broken at the packaged lane entry plus every post-mount composition seam except `onAuthoredAssetSwap` — the undercount ratchets toward `_shadowReceiverCount` hitting 0 with live receivers → `shadowMapActive` false → shadow refresh frozen on a stale texture.

- **lod F1 (H) — `addedSubtree` never reached the receiver port**: `residencyOptionsForBoundary`'s `syncPackagedBodyShadowPolicy` wrapper dropped arg 3 for every packaged commit (`visualOverrides` already passed it — silently dropped; `visualFactory`'s commit didn't pass it at all). The packaged subtree's minted `receiveShadow` flags were never tallied, and `noteRemoved(boundary)` at detach subtracted them anyway → monotonic downward ratchet per authored commit. Wrapper forwards `(root, entity, addedSubtree)`; the commit passes `packaged`.
- **lod F2 (H) — `swapTo` detach+attach dodged the tally**: the retained-root LOD swap only invalidated the caster policy; per-swap drift = prev−next receivers (promote direction undercounts → freeze class; demote overcounts → wasted renders). Renderer now mints `boundary.userData.shadowMeshNotes = {added, removed}` inside `_bindPresentationMesh`; `swapTo` calls them around the detach/attach.
- **lod F4 (M) — `refreshWholeShipLodFamily` silent detaches**: stale retained roots left with no note and no invalidate — phantom receivers plus a stale dirtySeq that kept a queued band-1 root re-forcing flags on meshes that no longer exist (the cold-link soundness contract). Removes route through the notes hook; `invalidateShadowCasterPolicy` runs when any root detached.
- **lod F3 (M-H) — partial-mark park kept verified-staged casters withheld indefinitely**: one forever-undrawable caster parked the whole root and the `continue` skipped the restore entirely — staged siblings stayed dark through every recheck/re-park cycle (a station loses all hull shadows). The park branch now runs the same restore, then re-forces `castShadow=false` on the leftover set only, and `_withheldDepthCasters` narrows to the leftovers so the cached re-stamp can't undo the restore.
- **lod F5 (L-M) — forever-undrawable re-park loop**: parked→recheck→collect→same unmarkables→withhold→re-park burned a collect+arm-slot+render-attempt every ~100 syncs per parked root forever. Consecutive undrawable parks now back the recheck cadence off geometrically (×2/cycle, cap 8×, ~96→768 syncs); the leftover still gets re-offered each recheck so a mesh drifting inside the key-light ortho self-heals — a permanent collector-skip flag would have left it dark forever. Cycle count resets on dirtySeq bumps, empty re-collects, detaches, and resolves.
- **lod F7 (L) — OFF→ON withheld already-staged casters**: the settings toggle's flag-only collect grabbed every castable mesh regardless of depth marks → all linked casters flickered dark for the drain. Now uses `collectUnstagedShadowCastersFlag` with the frame census sig — already-staged meshes never get withheld.
- **lod F6 (M) — scene-direct un-noted attach/detach family**: asteroid pool add/remove (the only pool minting true receivers), packaged instance-chunk publish/rollback/retire, and the bounded warm-root attach/park/release family all bypassed the notes. `scene.userData.shadowMeshNotes` minted once per renderer attach covers the pool/chunk files; the warm sites call `_noteShadowMeshAdded/Removed` inside their existing `parent`-guards so already-mounted roots can't double-count.

### W72 — census-keyed parked roots, exact-vs-recount bookkeeping, fused receiver walks, unavailable-release + launch-warm re-kick (all 4 lanes `saturated: false` — streak 0/3)

Merge: master `3e58e0cdb` in cleanly as `afa037e28` — upstream landed waves 60-69's twin work at `9431f548f` and re-pinned the 47a expected envelope to `892f88c9…`, so the golden assert passes on both trees again (the `54edce56` stale-pin canon red is gone).

- **lod F3 = boot F2 (M-H) — parked roots were census- and ortho-blind**: a park verdict minted with only `dirtySeq` + a recheck timer could never re-offer the root when the rendered light set re-signed (`lightCensusSignature` covers type-counts/layers/fog) or when the key-light ortho drifted over it — drawability flips that never bump `dirtySeq` left withheld casters dark past every recheck bound. Entries now stamp the frame census sig plus the ortho target's half-extent-quantized cell; either changing unparks into a fresh collect and clears the undrawable-cycle backoff (fresh world, fresh cadence).
- **lod F1 (M-H) — fixed-subtract notes at uncertain-mount seams**: the publish-rollback and `disposePreparedSectorBoundary` paths subtracted a counted subtree whose boundary mount is un-noted *by design* (the publish recount covers it) — before or after that recount the fixed subtract diverges from the scene. Both now call `markShadowReceiversDirty` — the recount re-derives exactly; exact notes stay only where mount-side counting is certain.
- **lod F2 (M) — chunk-mount symmetry restored**: `scene.add(mesh)` on the immediate-publish path never noted (retire did) — mounted-unlogged receivers; `finalizeRetiredInstanceChunk` now guards its note on `parent != null` before `removeFromParent` so an already-detached chunk can't double-subtract.
- **lod F4 (L) — opaqueMaterialBatch un-noted attach**: the batch mesh mounts `receiveShadow`-minted for the session's remainder; it now rides the `scene.userData.shadowMeshNotes` seam like every other scene-level attach.
- **lod F5 = hitches F4 (L) — arm tail inside `try`**: the requeue loop and `notePacedFrameSpend` debit sat inside the try body — a mid-slice throw both orphaned unrestored slice members (their pending slots were already dropped → withheld forever) and left the arm's spend uncharged. Both moved into the existing `finally` alongside the wedge-safe re-arm.
- **boot F1 (M) — OFF→ON whole-scene collect ran unbounded inside the settings click**: `collectUnstagedShadowCastersFlag([scene], sig)` with no `nodeBudget`. Now budgeted to `SHADOW_DEPTH_PASS_NODE_CAP`; `UNSTAGED_COLLECT_OVER_COVER` enqueues the scene's top-level roots into the pending queue so the arm's deadline-bounded per-root collects re-derive the real unstaged sets (queued roots withhold whole-subtree at their next sync).
- **boot F3 (L) — three dead shadow-map primes**: `compileShadowDepthPipelines({subjects: []})` at the Boot/Opening/PostOpening gates short-circuits on the empty-caster check before doing anything — pure dead ceremony plus a stale "allocate the shadow map" comment. Deleted.
- **boot F4 (L) — boundary `Object.assign` cloned per-root shadow bookkeeping**: `boundary.userData` inherited the fallback's policy record, dirty stamp, park-cycle counter, and its `shadowMeshNotes` seam — the wrong root answering the checked sync's reads and double-counting the tally. Four keys deleted at both boundary sites.
- **hitches F1 (H) — warm-root park paid two full subtree walks**: `traverse(count)` for diagnostics plus the tally's own `countShadowReceivers` inside `noteRemoved`. `countShadowReceivers` now takes an out-param emitting nodes+receivers in one walk; `_noteShadowMeshAdded/Removed` accept a pre-measured count; the park debits `notePacedFrameSpend` so the frame's slicers see the spend.
- **hitches F2 (M-H) — `onAuthoredAssetSwap` re-walked the incoming subtree**: `noteAdded(root)` traversed for flags the checked sync was about to read anyway. `syncShadowCasterPolicy` gained `preCountRoot` — an ancestor-climb membership tag measures the subtree's minted receivers inside its own traverse (~4 hops/object vs a second full walk); the swap consumes `out.preReceiverCount`. Packaged-body site stays — its scope subtree can't fuse cheaply.
- **hitches F5 (L) — `settings:changed` whole-emit marked the receiver tally unconditionally**: any video-setting emit (`key == null`) paid a whole-scene recount next frame for zero flag changes. Mark is now gated on the resolved `vd.shadows` value actually changing.
- **hitches F3 — deferred, documented**: teardown roster detach keeps the single post-fusion walk; an O(1) `sfWarmReceiverSum` stamp was judged risk>benefit (root-rebuild staleness classes vs halving one already-fused walk).
- **popin F2 (M-H) — `'unavailable'` was keep-claimed but mints for every swallowed null**: `loadAuthoredPart` resolves `null` for transient decode/fetch failures, consumer-inactive kills (including the warm's own `decodeWaitMs` deadline flipping `warm.building`), AND genuinely-missing files — all indistinguishable at the verdict sites, all pinned coverage for the whole run and unreachable by the deferred re-kick. Removed from `SWARM_WARM_CLAIMED_OUTCOMES`: released rows ride the bounded re-kick; a truly absent file's re-kick is a fast fetch failure, and the boundary vocabulary (`AUTHORED_ADMISSION_RETRY_MAX`) already treats `'unavailable'` as retry-eligible — the ledger was the stricter voice.
- **popin F1 (M) — launch warm had no same-window re-kick**: kicks recorded `unmarkOnRetriableOutcome` (release only); the W71 same-dwell re-kick existed only in the wave-2+ deferred lane, so a transient settle before round 1 popped inside the round. Ship/hulk exemplar kicks now mint `retriableKicks` closures; `finish()` runs ONE bounded re-kick pass (≤15s, ≤`budgetLeft-8s`) before `warm.building` drains — re-marking via `coveredMap` directly (the failed-marks guard owns the first strike), a second retriable settle releases for the next dwell.

### W73 — readmission-guarded launch re-kicks, coverage over-release fix, per-root toggle collect, template bookkeeping strips (all 4 lanes `saturated: false` — streak 0/3)

- **hitches F1 = boot F1 (H) — dead `userData.entityId` read minted `entity:null` on every depth-stage enqueue**: zero writers repo-wide (bind stamps `presentationEntityId`; pooled marks stamp `sfBoundEntityId`), so every OFF→ON/promotion enqueue resolved `allowCast:true` at restore — band-1 over-cast on out-of-ortho roots plus a forced revert traverse next sync. `shadowPolicyEntityOf` ancestor-walks `presentationEntityId ?? sfBoundEntityId ?? entityId ?? sfEntityId` (numeric-string coercion) — the same idiom the deadline-glass check runs — at both enqueue sites; boot adds `sfBoundEntityId` to the chain.
- **hitches F2 (M) — packaged-body commits now fuse the pre-count**: `syncPackagedBodyShadowPolicy` passes `preCountRoot: addedSubtree` into the checked sync and feeds `preReceiverCount` to `_noteShadowMeshAdded` — the same authored-swap seam contract; the packaged subtree IS mounted under the policy root so membership resolves trivially (the W72 "can't fuse cheaply" verdict was wrong about this site).
- **hitches F3 (M) — variant discriminant missed `alphaHash` + `vertexColors`**: `programCanon` treats alphaHash as program-discriminating and vertexColors changes the depth program's defines; a material flipping either under an interned variant re-read a stale mark as staged → cold link. `casterDepthVariant` now interns 10 bits.
- **popin F1 (H) — launch re-kick echoed the settled promise**: `startAuthoredUpgrade` returns `existing` verbatim for non-readmissible statuses — a raw `reKick(1)` on an `'unavailable'`/`'fallback-after-error'` boundary resolved the same status instantly (phantom strike-2, coverage released for a retry that never ran). The launch pass mirrors the deferred lane: `retryFailedAuthoredAdmission` marks the boundary first, and only genuinely-readmissible entries dispatch.
- **popin F2 (M-H) — deferred `unmark()` over-released shared coverage rows**: it subtracted `1 + retryMints` per enemyId even when a sync-failure `unmarkEnemy` already returned the initial mark and a retriable retry settle already returned its mint — releasing foreign claims on the shared ref-counted row (the launch re-kick's +1s land on the same map). Now tracks `retryReleasedCounts` and subtracts only still-outstanding shares.
- **popin F3 (M) — re-kick mints blocked the deferred fresh filter for a whole dwell**: a deferred warm snapshotting inside the re-kick window skipped a row held only by an in-flight strike-2; its release arrived after the snapshot — the archetype spawned unwarmed that wave. Re-kick mints now also count into `_swarmWarmReKickClaims` (nulled with the coverage map on run:ended); `fresh` takes any row whose coverage is entirely outstanding re-kick claims — a bounded duplicated exemplar behind the armory beats a guaranteed in-round compose.
- **popin F4 (M) — in-flight kicks that settled retriable during the race got no strike**: `entry.result === undefined` entries were skipped by the one-time census, so a kick landing retriable inside `Promise.race` released its row with no second attempt. `collectReKicks` now runs a bounded re-scan after the first race (`reKicked` flag per entry; ≤`budgetLeft-2s`/8s tail bound).
- **popin F5 (M) — player-hull top-up kick had no warm-owner guards**: `_topUpEarlyCrucibleWarmPlayerSpec` kicked without `isResidencyOwnerActive` or a retriableKicks entry — the only launch kick that could pin residency work past `warm.building` and never got a strike-2. Now closure-mints attempts like the roster kicks (`enemyId:null` — the player hull carries no coverage row; the re-kick pass runs it with claim math skipped).
- **popin F6 = lod F1 (H) — OFF→ON over-cover enqueue never invalidated band-1 roots**: roots promoted while shadows were OFF keep `dirtySeq=0`/`stampedSeq=-1`, so the queued arm's collectGate (`dirtySeq > stampedSeq`) read them as certified → never-staged casters drew live on the next presented refresh. The enqueue now pays the same `invalidateShadowCasterPolicy` + `STAGE_SELF_DIRTY_KEY` stamp the withheld path does.
- **lod F2 (M) — enable collect minted an OFF-era census**: `_stageShadowDepthOnSettingEnable` ran while `key.castShadow` was still false (the flip lands inside `_syncShadowMapEnabled`, called after) — every ON-era mark compared stale → mass withhold of already-linked casters. `_syncShadowMapEnabled` now runs before the enable stage and nulls `_shadowCensusMemo` on each `castShadow` write, so no seq-memo survives a mid-frame light flip.
- **lod F3 (L) — over-cover discarded visited-prefix coverage**: the whole-scene flag collect aborted on the node budget and the fallback queued ALL of `scene.children` — provably-clean roots (light shells, pools, parallax) each paid an arm collect and a collectGate eval per sync. The collect now walks top-level roots per-call on the shared budget: completed-empty roots skip entirely, completed-with-unstaged roots take the per-mesh withhold, only the in-flight root + unvisited siblings queue whole-subtree.
- **boot F2 (M-H) — place-prop boundary cloned per-root shadow bookkeeping**: `wrapPlacePropWithAuthoredPart`'s `Object.assign(boundary.userData, …)` lacked the ship/station wraps' 4-key strip — a shared policy record could satisfy the other's dirty/stamp reads and double-count the notes seam. Same four deletes added.
- **boot F3+F4 (M) — flight-template clones carried source bookkeeping + entity stamps**: `dropFlightTemplateDynamicUserData` kept `__spacefaceShadowCasterPolicyV1`, `__spacefaceDepthStageSelfDirty`, `sfDepthUndrawableCycles`, `sfDepthMark`, `shadowMeshNotes`, `presentationEntityId`, `sfBoundEntityId`, `sfStableEntityKey` — a synced source's clone read certified-dirty on casts its subtree never proved, and live `morphAttributes` refs pinned whole attribute arrays into the JSON clone walk. All nine keys now drop in the shared pass (both the pre-clone projection and the instance clear ride it).
- **boot F5 (L) — survival post-settle sweep lacked the cookOverBudget shrink**: `SF_SurvivalPostSettleShadowDepth` always paid `[scene, livingHullRoot]`; `SF_CookRockPoolShadowDepth` already drops its scene leg when the window closes. Parity: scene leg only while `prepareNow() < settleDeadline` — the living-hull subtree still primes.

### W74 — census-mutation epoch, wedgeable warmBuilding bound, ghost clone scrub, sliced deferred builds (all 4 lanes `saturated: false` — streak 0/3)

- **hitches F1 (H) — `warmBuilding` wedgeable on two reachable paths**: (a) `finish()` skipped wholesale when the cook went over-budget → the settle arm never engaged → `warm.root.userData.warmBuilding` stayed true → every `_parkBoundedWarmRoots` sweep skipped the ~13,845-node hidden root (~11 ms/frame for the run) and `warm.building` pinned decode leases live via `isResidencyOwnerActive`; (b) `settleWarmBuilding` was a bare `Promise.allSettled` re-armed on growth — one never-settling attach wedged the flag identically, and the deferred warm's bare `allSettled` additionally wedged `swarmDeferredWarm.pending` (10 s draft-gate stall per "Keep current loadout" click). New `WARM_BUILDING_SETTLE_DEADLINE_MS` (30 s absolute): `_armCrucibleWarmBuildingSettle` races `allSettled` vs the remaining window, re-arms on growth only before the deadline, then drains both flags — the over-budget path mirrors finish()'s early `building` clear and arms the same settle. The deferred warm's settle + its same-dwell re-kick pass share the deadline (timed-out rows release coverage like retriable settles but earn no re-kick — their kicks may still be in flight; late attaches drop silently via `boundaryBelongsToScene`, the documented trade confined to the hang).
- **lod F1 (M) — census mutations that never kill the same-seq memo**: `syncVisiblePointLightBudget`'s stand-in mount/release and `disposeObject`'s subtree teardown can change the rendered light set mid-seq after `_shadowCensusMemo` minted — stale sigs served to the parked-release/stamp consumers. `precompile.js` can't import `renderer.js` (cycle), so `shadowCensusMutationEpoch` + `noteShadowCensusLightMutation` live on the shared leaf (`shadowDepthAdmission.js`): the memo folds the epoch into validity — bump sites are the two point-light legs and an `isLight` probe inside `disposeObject`'s existing traverse. `_ensureKeyLightShadows`' OFF-leg is already order-safe; no change.
- **lod F2 (L) — `sfBoundEntityId` cleared at unbind**: its own contract is "the entity bound right now" — a mesh leaving the presentation world kept its previous owner's mark, letting `shadowPolicyEntityOf` resolve a stale entity on a recycled parked root (wrong-entity, safe direction, self-healing — now closed). Cleared only when the mark names the unbinding entity (or no id is known) — a mismatched mark belongs to a different binding's lifecycle. `presentationEntityId` stays: `partsLibrary.boundaryLiveEntity` needs it for save-restore owner resolution.
- **boot F1 (H) — crucibleGhost's `clone(true)` JSON round-tripped live userData per node**: `sfDepthMark` geometry+material refs, the quadratic `renderPackageInstance` subtree, identity stamps (`presentationEntityId`/`sfBoundEntityId`/`sfStableEntityKey` resolving the ghost to the live entity), shadow bookkeeping, and every function disposer serialized into dead `{}` records — a presented-frame dump plus mangled `casterDepthMarkCurrent` (ghost permanently reads unstaged). `ensureClone` now swaps each node's `userData` for a plain-data projection (drop-list + functions/symbols/non-plain prototypes pruned) for the clone's duration, restores after, and forces `matrixAutoUpdate = true` on the cloned root (a source frozen while hidden mints an unposeable ghost).
- **popin F1 (M) — claimed re-kick settles left stale `_swarmWarmReKickClaims`**: `releaseClaim` decremented both maps but ran only on non-claiming settles → a successful strike-2 read `covered=1 <= claims=1` as still-in-flight → the deferred warm duplicated the full per-archetype cohort at the next dwell. `releaseClaim` split: claims decrement on every settle, covered decrement stays claimed-gated.
- **popin F2 (S) — sync `reKick(1)` throw aborted the whole pass**: an `enqueueBoundaryUpgrade` construction throw propagated out of `collectReKicks` — aborted every remaining strike, leaked the mints, rejected `finish()` mid-cook leaving `warmBuilding` stuck. Now `try/catch` → `releaseClaim(enemyId); continue` — same guard `kickAuthoredBoundaryUpgrade` already runs.
- **hitches F2 (M) — deferred-warm build prefix was one sync block**: spec enum → 2×witness `vf.build` per archetype → hulk specs → kick dispatches ran unbounded inside a `run:transitioned`/`run:wavePlanned` emit (the fallback can land in the round's first presented frames). The build is now an async continuation yielding between exemplars at 4 ms slices (marks still atomic upfront; a thrown build still unmarks+tears down once; settle legs gate on the build's success flag) and debits `notePacedFrameSpend`.
- **hitches F3 (L) — `_mintDeferredPaletteSubjects` scanned the whole decoded-parts registry synchronously**: per-record `split('::')` + regex `normalizeFile`, no yields — same 4 ms slice pattern + ledger debit.
